title: "Sustainable Energy — Without the Hot Air: A Quiz Companion"
author: terlingua-team
summary: An unofficial self-test companion to David MacKay's free book Sustainable Energy — without the hot air (UIT Cambridge, 2009). Each chapter mirrors a chapter of the book and each section mirrors one of its sections, with page pointers, learning objectives and quiz questions whose hints quote the book or its errata. Chapters 1–32 match the book's numbering; chapters 33–40 are technical chapters A–H and 41–43 are the data chapters I–K. Read the book free at withouthotair.com.
date: 2026-11-08
tags:
  - energy
  - climate
  - physics
  - science
  - quiz

FILE: export.sh
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# ===========================================================================
# export.sh — Export git-tracked project files to docs/llm/dump.txt
#
# Blog posts (content/blog/*.md): only front matter is included; the
# markdown body is omitted to save context space. Every other git-tracked
# file is exported in full.
#
# Excluded from export:
#   - The docs/ directory itself (to avoid recursion / stale LLM context)
#   - Binary files (images, fonts, etc.)
#
# Usage:
#   bash export.sh            # writes docs/llm/dump.txt
#   bash export.sh -o out.txt # writes to a custom path
# ===========================================================================

set -euo pipefail

# ── Configuration ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

OUTPUT_FILE="docs/llm/dump.txt"
BLOG_DIR="content/blog"
BOOK_DIR="content/books"
SEPARATOR="-------------------------------------------------------------------------------"
WIDE_SEP="==============================================================================="

# Binary file extensions to skip (lowercase comparison)
BINARY_EXTENSIONS="png jpg jpeg gif ico bmp svg webp woff woff2 ttf eot otf mp3 mp4 wav ogg pdf zip tar gz"

# ── Parse arguments ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
    case "$1" in
        -o|--output)
            OUTPUT_FILE="$2"
            shift 2
            ;;
        -h|--help)
            echo "Usage: bash export.sh [-o OUTPUT_FILE]"
            echo "  Exports git-tracked project files to docs/llm/dump.txt"
            echo "  Blog posts include front matter only (body omitted)."
            exit 0
            ;;
        *)
            echo "Unknown argument: $1" >&2
            exit 1
            ;;
    esac
done

# ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

is_binary_extension() {
    local file="$1"
    local ext="${file##*.}"
    ext="${ext,,}" # lowercase
    for bin_ext in $BINARY_EXTENSIONS; do
        if [[ "$ext" == "$bin_ext" ]]; then
            return 0
        fi
    done
    return 1
}

is_blog_post() {
    local file="$1"
    [[ "$file" == ${BLOG_DIR}/*.md ]]
}

is_book() {
    local file="$1"
    [[ "$file" == ${BOOK_DIR}/*.md ]]
}

is_excluded_dir() {
    local file="$1"
    [[ "$file" == docs/* ]]
}

# Extract only the YAML front matter (between first --- and second ---).
# Prints the front matter block including delimiters.
extract_front_matter() {
    local file="$1"
    local in_front_matter=0
    local found_start=0

    while IFS= read -r line; do
        if [[ "$found_start" -eq 0 && "$line" == "---" ]]; then
            found_start=1
            in_front_matter=1
            echo "$line"
            continue
        fi
        if [[ "$in_front_matter" -eq 1 && "$line" == "---" ]]; then
            echo "$line"
            return
        fi
        if [[ "$in_front_matter" -eq 1 ]]; then
            echo "$line"
        fi
    done < "$file"
}

# Extract only the first line starting with "Title:"
extract_book_title() {
    local file="$1"
    while IFS= read -r line || [[ -n "$line" ]]; do
        if [[ "$line" == Title:* ]]; then
            echo "$line"
            return
        fi
    done < "$file"
}

# ── Collect git-tracked files ────────────────────────────────────────────────

mapfile -t ALL_FILES < <(git ls-files --cached --others --exclude-standard | sort)

# ── Counters ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

total_files=0
blog_front_matter_only=0
book_front_matter_only=0
full_content_files=0
binary_skipped=0

# ── Ensure output directory exists ───────────────────────────────────────────

mkdir -p "$(dirname "$OUTPUT_FILE")"

# ── Write output ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

{
    # Header
    echo "$WIDE_SEP"
    echo "PROJECT EXPORT: terlingua.github.io"
    echo "DATE: $(date -u '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC')"
    echo "$WIDE_SEP"
    echo ""

    # Directory tree
    echo "$WIDE_SEP"
    echo "DIRECTORY TREE (all git-tracked files)"
    echo "$WIDE_SEP"
    echo ""

    # Use tree if available, otherwise fall back to a simple listing
    if command -v tree &>/dev/null; then
        # Build a temp file list for tree --fromfile
        printf '%s\n' "${ALL_FILES[@]}" | tree --fromfile --charset utf-8 -a 2>/dev/null || {
            # Fallback: just print the sorted file list as an indented tree
            echo "."
            printf '%s\n' "${ALL_FILES[@]}" | sed 's|^|├── |'
        }
    else
        echo "."
        printf '%s\n' "${ALL_FILES[@]}" | sed 's|^|├── |'
    fi

    echo ""

    # File contents
    for file in "${ALL_FILES[@]}"; do
        # Skip files in docs/ directory
        if is_excluded_dir "$file"; then
            continue
        fi

        # Skip binary files
        if is_binary_extension "$file"; then
            binary_skipped=$((binary_skipped + 1))
            total_files=$((total_files + 1))
            continue
        fi

        # Skip the export script itself
        if [[ "$file" == "export.sh" ]]; then
            # Still include it — it's useful context for the LLM
            :
        fi

        echo ""
        echo "$SEPARATOR"

        if is_blog_post "$file"; then
            # Blog post: front matter only
            echo "FILE: ${file}  [FRONT MATTER ONLY — body omitted to save context space]"
            echo "$SEPARATOR"
            extract_front_matter "$file"
            echo ""
            blog_front_matter_only=$((blog_front_matter_only + 1))
        elif is_book "$file"; then
            # Blog post: front matter only
            echo "FILE: ${file}  [FRONT MATTER ONLY — body omitted to save context space]"
            echo "$SEPARATOR"
            extract_book_title "$file"
            echo ""
            book_front_matter_only=$((book_front_matter_only + 1))
        else
            # All other files: full content
            echo "FILE: ${file}"
            echo "$SEPARATOR"
            cat "$file"
            echo ""
            full_content_files=$((full_content_files + 1))
        fi

        total_files=$((total_files + 1))
    done

    # Footer
    echo ""
    echo "$WIDE_SEP"
    echo "EXPORT COMPLETED"
    echo "Total Files Exported: ${total_files}"
    echo "  Blog posts (front matter only): ${blog_front_matter_only}"
    echo "  Book posts (front matter only): ${book_front_matter_only}"
    echo "  Full content files: ${full_content_files}"
    echo "  Binary files skipped: ${binary_skipped}"
    echo "Output File: ${OUTPUT_FILE}"
    echo "$WIDE_SEP"

} > "$OUTPUT_FILE"

echo "Export complete: ${OUTPUT_FILE}"
echo "  Total files: ${total_files}"
echo "  Blog posts (front matter only): ${blog_front_matter_only}"
echo "  Book posts (front matter only): ${book_front_matter_only}"
echo "  Full content files: ${full_content_files}"
echo "  Binary files skipped: ${binary_skipped}"


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: .github/workflows/deploy.yml
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
name: Deploy to GitHub Pages

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 6 * * *'
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read
  pages: write
  id-token: write

concurrency:
  group: pages
  cancel-in-progress: false

env:
  DOTNET_NOLOGO: true
  DOTNET_CLI_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT: true
  TESTINGPLATFORM_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT: true

jobs:
  build-and-deploy:
    environment:
      name: github-pages
      url: ${{ steps.deployment.outputs.page_url }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - name: Setup .NET
        uses: actions/setup-dotnet@v6
        with:
          dotnet-version: '11.0.x'

      - name: Restore
        run: dotnet restore

      - name: Process content
        run: dotnet run --project tools/ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor --no-restore

      - name: Restore audio cache
        id: audio-cache
        uses: actions/cache@v6
        with:
          path: .audio-cache
          key: audio-v1-${{ hashFiles('content/blog/**/*.md') }}
          restore-keys: |
            audio-v1-

      - name: Copy cached audio to output
        run: |
          mkdir -p src/ObserverMagazine.Web/wwwroot/blog-data
          shopt -s nullglob
          files=(.audio-cache/*.mp3 .audio-cache/audio-manifest.json)
          if (( ${#files[@]} )); then cp "${files[@]}" src/ObserverMagazine.Web/wwwroot/blog-data/; fi
          echo "Restored ${#files[@]} cached audio file(s)"

      - name: Setup Python
        if: steps.audio-cache.outputs.cache-hit != 'true'
        uses: actions/setup-python@v7
        with:
          python-version: '3.13'
          cache: pip
          cache-dependency-path: tools/requirements-audio.txt

      - name: Install system dependencies
        if: steps.audio-cache.outputs.cache-hit != 'true'
        run: sudo apt-get update && sudo apt-get install -y espeak-ng ffmpeg

      - name: Cache Hugging Face model
        if: steps.audio-cache.outputs.cache-hit != 'true'
        uses: actions/cache@v6
        with:
          path: ~/.cache/huggingface
          key: hf-kitten-tts-nano-0.8

      - name: Install Python audio dependencies
        if: steps.audio-cache.outputs.cache-hit != 'true'
        run: pip install onnxruntime -r tools/requirements-audio.txt

      - name: Generate TTS audio
        if: steps.audio-cache.outputs.cache-hit != 'true'
        run: python tools/generate_audio.py --content-dir content/blog --output-dir src/ObserverMagazine.Web/wwwroot/blog-data --voice Bella --model KittenML/kitten-tts-nano-0.8

      - name: Update audio cache
        if: steps.audio-cache.outputs.cache-hit != 'true'
        run: |
          mkdir -p .audio-cache
          shopt -s nullglob
          files=(src/ObserverMagazine.Web/wwwroot/blog-data/*.mp3 src/ObserverMagazine.Web/wwwroot/blog-data/audio-manifest.json)
          if (( ${#files[@]} )); then cp "${files[@]}" .audio-cache/; fi
          echo "Cached ${#files[@]} audio file(s)"

      - name: Test
        run: dotnet test --solution ObserverMagazine.slnx --no-restore

      - name: Publish
        run: dotnet publish src/ObserverMagazine.Web/ObserverMagazine.Web.csproj -c Release -o publish

      - name: Prepare site
        run: cp publish/wwwroot/index.html publish/wwwroot/404.html

      - name: Upload artifact
        uses: actions/upload-pages-artifact@v5
        with:
          path: publish/wwwroot

      - name: Deploy
        id: deployment
        uses: actions/deploy-pages@v5

FILE: README.md
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Terlingua Magazine

A free, open-source Blazor WebAssembly application deployed to GitHub Pages.

Built with **.NET 11** and **C# 15**. Targets the current stable releases of evergreen browsers only
(Chrome, Edge, Firefox, Safari); there are no polyfills, vendor prefixes or legacy fallbacks.

## Features

- **Blog** — Write posts in Markdown with YAML front matter; compiled to static JSON at build time
- **RSS Feed** — Auto-generated `feed.xml`
- **Books & Quizzes** — Multi-chapter books with single- and multiple-answer quizzes; reading progress stored in the browser (localStorage)
- **Responsive Tables** — Sortable, filterable data tables that work on mobile
- **Master-Detail Flow** — Click a list item to view full details
- **OpenTelemetry-ready Logging** — Structured logging via `ILogger`
- **Full Test Suite** — bUnit component tests + xUnit.net v3 integration tests, running on Microsoft.Testing.Platform

## Quick Start

`global.json` asks for SDK `11.0.100-rc.3` or any later 11.0.1xx SDK, so the Fedora RC 3 SDK works
locally and CI uses the latest .NET 11 GA SDK.

```bash
# Process blog posts and books
dotnet run --project tools/ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor

# Run the app locally
dotnet run --project src/ObserverMagazine.Web

# Run all tests
dotnet test
```

## Books

Books live under `content/books/` and are compiled by the content processor into
`wwwroot/book-data/` (git-ignored) at build time.

```
content/books/
  blazor-basics/                  # book slug (lowercase, digits, hyphens)
    book.yml                      # title, author, summary, date, tags, image, draft
    01-getting-started/           # NN-chapter-slug; the number sets the order
      chapter.yml                 # title, summary, draft
      01-what-is-blazor.md        # NN-subchapter-slug.md
      02-project-structure.md
    02-components/
      ...
```

Chapters and subchapters are numbered 1, 2, 3… in the order of their prefixes, so gaps
(`01`, `05`, `10`) are fine. Drafts and future-dated books are skipped.

### Subchapter format

```markdown
---
title: What is Blazor WebAssembly?
questions:
  - id: runtime                   # optional, defaults to q1, q2…; keep stable once published
    prompt: Where does the C# code run?
    explanation: Shown after a correct answer.   # optional
    options:
      - text: On the server
      - text: In the browser
        correct: true
  - id: hosting
    prompt: Which statements are true?
    type: multiple                # optional: single | multiple
    options:
      - id: static                # optional, defaults to a, b, c…
        text: It can be served from GitHub Pages
        correct: true
      - text: It needs ASP.NET Core on the server
---
Markdown body…
```

- `type` is inferred when omitted: more than one correct option means `multiple`.
  Set `type: multiple` explicitly to use checkboxes even when only one option is correct.
- Prompts, options and explanations support Markdown (inline code, emphasis, links).
- Question and option ids are what reader progress is keyed on. Changing them resets
  progress for that question.
- Invalid content (no correct option, fewer than two options, unnumbered files, missing
  `chapter.yml`, bad YAML, duplicate ids) fails the build with a list of errors.

### Library

| Book | Slug | Focus |
|------|------|-------|
| Nutrition Science: What We Know and What We Don't | `nutrition-science` | Evidence-graded diet and nutrition: energy, macro- and micronutrients, patterns, disease, life stages, food safety, myths |
| .NET 10 and Modern C# | `modern-dotnet` | SDK, C# 14, async, hosting, minimal APIs, EF Core 10, architecture, testing, AOT |
| Timeless .NET: Modern C# 14 and .NET 10 Without the Vestiges | `timeless-dotnet` | Records, C# 14, tasks and async, cancellation tokens, DI, explicit mapping, modern Dapper, stored procedures vs parameterized SQL, xUnit v3 on MTP, .NET 11 and C# 15; spotting anachronisms, dogma, factoids and vestiges |
| Modern Web Development with Blazor | `modern-blazor` | Render modes, component reuse, forms, routing, state, interop, security, Hybrid |
| SOLID Principles in C# | `solid-principles` | The five principles, refactorings and trade-offs |
| Clean Code in C# | `clean-code` | Naming, functions, errors, classes, tests, smells, refactoring |
| Working Effectively with Legacy Code: A Companion | `working-effectively-with-legacy-code` | Chapter-by-chapter quizzes for Feathers' book: seams, characterization tests, sprouting and wrapping, the 24 dependency-breaking techniques |
| Dapper: Fast, Honest Data Access | `dapper` | Queries, parameters, mapping, transactions, architecture, testing |
| The Databricks Data Intelligence Platform | `databricks-platform` | Spark, Delta Lake, Unity Catalog, pipelines, jobs, SQL, MLflow, GenAI |
| Databricks Fundamentals: The Platform Without the Brochure | `databricks-fundamentals` | Companion to the Databricks Fundamentals course: lakehouse, control and compute planes, DBUs and cost, Lakeflow, Databricks SQL and Genie, MLflow and serving, Unity Catalog, workspace, lock-in and when not to use Databricks |
| Generative AI Fundamentals: How It Works, Where It Fails | `generative-ai-fundamentals` | Companion to the Generative AI Fundamentals course: tokens, embeddings, training and alignment, decoding, hallucination, prompting and injection, RAG, fine-tuning, evaluation, economics, regulation, GenAI on Databricks |
| AI Agent Fundamentals: From Loops to Production | `ai-agent-fundamentals` | Companion to the AI Agent Fundamentals course: tools, memory, planning, workflow patterns, multi-agent systems, MCP and A2A, Agent Bricks, evaluation, the lethal trifecta, ROI, agents in .NET with Microsoft.Extensions.AI, Agent Framework, MCP and C# 15 |
| Microsoft Azure for .NET Developers | `microsoft-azure` | Identity, compute, data, messaging, networking, Bicep, governance |
| Amazon Web Services for Builders | `amazon-web-services` | IAM, compute, storage, databases, integration, VPC, IaC, cost |
| Blazor WebAssembly Basics | `blazor-basics` | A short introduction |

Content guidelines for new sections:

- Open with a "Learning objectives" list and keep each section focused on one concept
- Prefer small, compilable code snippets with a language on every code fence
- Write three or more questions per section, mixing single- and multiple-answer questions,
  with an `explanation` on every question
- Vary the position of the correct option; the UI does not shuffle options
- Companions to published books follow the original chapter and section order, quote only short passages, and put each quotation inside “ ” in the `explanation` so it can be checked against the source
- Non-software books may grade claims by certainty; `nutrition-science` uses Established, Strong, Uncertain and Not supported, and cites sources at the end of each section
- Quote option text that YAML would read as a boolean, number or null (`"Yes"`, `"50"`), and avoid `yes`, `no`, `null`, `true` and `false` as ids
- Quote YAML values that contain `: `, ` #` or start with a backtick or `@`

### Progress and analytics

- A section is complete when every question has been answered correctly, or, if it has
  no questions, once it has been opened.
- Progress is saved per book in `localStorage` under `book:<slug>`. If storage is
  unavailable (blocked storage, quota) the app falls back to in-memory storage and tells
  the reader that progress will not persist.
- When the analytics backend is reachable, the reader count of a book and of each
  section is shown. A reader is counted once per book and once per section, using the
  local progress record to avoid counting repeat visits. Quiz answers are sent as
  interaction events. Nothing is shown and nothing breaks when the backend is down.

## Front end

- The site ships no JavaScript of its own. The only interop is `localStorage` through
  `LocalBrowserStorage` (`IBrowserStorage`), which wraps every call in `try`/`catch` as
  analyzer BL0016 requires.
- Code blocks are highlighted at build time by `SyntaxHighlighter` in the content processor,
  plugged into Markdig as `SyntaxHighlightingExtension`. The `hl-*` colour classes live in
  `app.css`.
- Horizontally scrolling code blocks show edge shadows with pure CSS
  (`background-attachment: local`).
- Themes are CSS custom properties. `MainLayout` renders `data-theme` on `.page` and saves the
  choice in `localStorage` (`observer-theme`). The default `system` theme uses `light-dark()`
  and `color-scheme`, so it follows the OS setting live. The loading screen always uses the
  system theme.
- The audio player uses the browser's native controls, including playback speed, and stays
  hidden until the MP3's metadata loads.
- `OverrideHtmlAssetPlaceholders` fingerprints the framework files (`dotnet.js` is published as
  `dotnet.<hash>.js`) and fills the `<script type="importmap">` placeholder in `index.html`,
  which is how `blazor.webassembly.js` finds them. Keep that placeholder, and don't modify files
  under `_framework` after publish, because the import map can carry integrity hashes for them.
  A 404 for `/_framework/dotnet.js` on the live site is expected.
- GitHub Pages deep links work because CI copies the published `index.html` to `404.html`.

## Testing

Tests run on **Microsoft.Testing.Platform (MTP)**, not VSTest. The opt-in lives in
`global.json`:

```json
{ "test": { "runner": "Microsoft.Testing.Platform" } }
```

Test projects are ordinary executables, so each one can also be run on its own:

```bash
# Whole solution
dotnet test --solution ObserverMagazine.slnx

# One project
dotnet test --project tests/ObserverMagazine.Web.Tests

# Filter by class, or by trait
dotnet test --filter-class ObserverMagazine.Web.Tests.Services.BlogServiceTests
dotnet test --filter-trait Category=Integration

# Run a test project directly, no dotnet test involved
dotnet run --project tests/ObserverMagazine.Integration.Tests
```

Run `dotnet test -?` from the repo root for the full option list, which is built
from whichever MTP extensions the test projects reference.

xUnit configuration for MTP lives in `testconfig.json` next to a test project
(not `xunit.runner.json`, which is the VSTest-era file).

| Component | Technology | License |

@if (Author is not null)
{
    <div class="author-card">
        @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Author.Avatar))
        {
            <img class="author-avatar" src="@Author.Avatar" alt="@Author.Name" loading="lazy" />
        }
        <div class="author-info">
            <strong class="author-name">@Author.Name</strong>
            @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Author.Bio))
            {
                <p class="author-bio">@Author.Bio</p>
            }
            @if (Author.Socials is { Count: > 0 })
            {
                <div class="author-socials">
                    @foreach (var (platform, handle) in Author.Socials)
                    {
                        <a href="@GetSocialUrl(platform, handle)"
                           target="_blank"
                           rel="noopener noreferrer"
                           class="author-social-link"
                           title="@GetDisplayName(platform)">
                            @GetDisplayName(platform)
                        </a>
                    }
                </div>
            }
        </div>
    </div>
}

@code {
    [Parameter] public AuthorProfile? Author { get; set; }

    private static string GetDisplayName(string platform) => platform.ToLowerInvariant() switch
    {
        "twitter" or "x" => "X / Twitter",
        "github" => "GitHub",
        "gitlab" => "GitLab",
        "bitbucket" => "Bitbucket",
        "linkedin" => "LinkedIn",
        "bluesky" => "Bluesky",
        "mastodon" => "Mastodon",
        "threads" => "Threads",
        "facebook" => "Facebook",
        "instagram" => "Instagram",
        "tiktok" => "TikTok",
        "youtube" => "YouTube",
        "twitch" => "Twitch",
        "reddit" => "Reddit",
        "pinterest" => "Pinterest",
        "tumblr" => "Tumblr",
        "snapchat" => "Snapchat",
        "discord" => "Discord",
        "telegram" => "Telegram",
        "whatsapp" => "WhatsApp",
        "signal" => "Signal",
        "medium" => "Medium",
        "substack" => "Substack",
        "devto" or "dev.to" => "DEV",
        "hashnode" => "Hashnode",
        "stackoverflow" => "Stack Overflow",
        "hackernews" => "Hacker News",
        "codepen" => "CodePen",
        "dribbble" => "Dribbble",
        "behance" => "Behance",
        "flickr" => "Flickr",
        "500px" => "500px",
        "vimeo" => "Vimeo",
        "soundcloud" => "SoundCloud",
        "bandcamp" => "Bandcamp",
        "spotify" => "Spotify",
        "goodreads" => "Goodreads",
        "patreon" => "Patreon",
        "kofi" or "ko-fi" => "Ko-fi",
        "buymeacoffee" => "Buy Me a Coffee",
        "wordpress" => "WordPress",
        "quora" => "Quora",
        "keybase" => "Keybase",
        "orcid" => "ORCID",
        "researchgate" => "ResearchGate",
        "figma" => "Figma",
        "notion" => "Notion",
        "producthunt" => "Product Hunt",
        "angellist" => "AngelList",
        "crunchbase" => "Crunchbase",
        "linktree" => "Linktree",

    private static string GetSocialUrl(string platform, string handle) => platform.ToLowerInvariant() switch
    {
        // Developer platforms
        "github" => $"https://github.com/{handle}",
        "gitlab" => $"https://gitlab.com/{handle}",
        "bitbucket" => $"https://bitbucket.org/{handle}",
        "codepen" => $"https://codepen.io/{handle}",
        "devto" or "dev.to" => $"https://dev.to/{handle}",
        "hashnode" => $"https://{handle}.hashnode.dev",
        "stackoverflow" => $"https://stackoverflow.com/users/{handle}",
        "hackernews" => $"https://news.ycombinator.com/user?id={handle}",

        // Major social networks
        "twitter" or "x" => $"https://x.com/{handle}",
        "facebook" => $"https://facebook.com/{handle}",
        "instagram" => $"https://instagram.com/{handle}",
        "linkedin" => $"https://linkedin.com/in/{handle}",
        "threads" => $"https://threads.net/@{handle}",
        "bluesky" => handle.Contains('.') ? $"https://bsky.app/profile/{handle}" : $"https://bsky.app/profile/{handle}.bsky.social",
        "mastodon" => handle.StartsWith("http") ? handle : $"https://mastodon.social/{handle}",
        "tiktok" => $"https://tiktok.com/@{handle}",
        "snapchat" => $"https://snapchat.com/add/{handle}",
        "pinterest" => $"https://pinterest.com/{handle}",
        "tumblr" => $"https://{handle}.tumblr.com",
        "reddit" => $"https://reddit.com/user/{handle}",

        // Video / streaming
        "youtube" => $"https://youtube.com/@{handle}",
        "twitch" => $"https://twitch.tv/{handle}",
        "vimeo" => $"https://vimeo.com/{handle}",

        // Audio / music
        "spotify" => $"https://open.spotify.com/user/{handle}",
        "soundcloud" => $"https://soundcloud.com/{handle}",
        "bandcamp" => $"https://{handle}.bandcamp.com",

        // Design / creative
        "dribbble" => $"https://dribbble.com/{handle}",
        "behance" => $"https://behance.net/{handle}",
        "flickr" => $"https://flickr.com/photos/{handle}",
        "500px" => $"https://500px.com/p/{handle}",
        "figma" => $"https://figma.com/@{handle}",

        // Writing / blogging
        "medium" => $"https://medium.com/@{handle}",
        "substack" => $"https://{handle}.substack.com",
        "wordpress" => $"https://{handle}.wordpress.com",

        // Messaging
        "discord" => handle.StartsWith("http") ? handle : $"https://discord.gg/{handle}",
        "telegram" => $"https://t.me/{handle}",
        "whatsapp" => $"https://wa.me/{handle}",
        "signal" => $"https://signal.me/#p/{handle}",

        // Monetisation
        "patreon" => $"https://patreon.com/{handle}",
        "kofi" or "ko-fi" => $"https://ko-fi.com/{handle}",
        "buymeacoffee" => $"https://buymeacoffee.com/{handle}",

        // Books / academia
        "goodreads" => $"https://goodreads.com/user/show/{handle}",
        "orcid" => $"https://orcid.org/{handle}",
        "researchgate" => $"https://researchgate.net/profile/{handle}",

        // Professional / other
        "quora" => $"https://quora.com/profile/{handle}",
        "keybase" => $"https://keybase.io/{handle}",
        "producthunt" => $"https://producthunt.com/@{handle}",
        "angellist" => $"https://angel.co/u/{handle}",
        "crunchbase" => $"https://crunchbase.com/person/{handle}",
        "notion" => $"https://notion.so/{handle}",
        "linktree" => $"https://linktr.ee/{handle}",
        "calendly" => $"https://calendly.com/{handle}",

        // Default: "website"/"web"/"homepage" and any unknown platform
        _ => handle.StartsWith("http") ? handle : $"https://{handle}"
    };
}

FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Components/BlogTtsPlayer.razor
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
@inject IAnalyticsService Analytics
@inject ILogger<BlogTtsPlayer> Logger

<div class="tts-player" hidden="@(!_available)">
    <span class="tts-icon" aria-hidden="true">🔊</span>
    <span class="tts-label">Listen to this post</span>

    <audio controls
           preload="metadata"
           src="@AudioUrl"
           @onloadedmetadata="OnLoadedMetadata"
           @onplay="OnPlay"
           @onpause="OnPause"
           @onended="OnEnded"></audio>

    <a class="tts-direct-link" href="@AudioUrl" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"
       title="Open audio file directly in a new tab">
        Open audio ↗
    </a>
</div>

@code {
    [Parameter, EditorRequired]
    public string Slug { get; set; } = "";

    private string? _currentSlug;
    private bool _available;

    private string AudioUrl => $"blog-data/{Slug}.mp3";

    protected override void OnParametersSet()
    {
        if (Slug == _currentSlug)
        {
            return;
        }

        _currentSlug = Slug;
        _available = false;
    }

    private void OnLoadedMetadata() => _available = true;

    private async Task OnPlay()
    {
        Logger.LogInformation("TTS play: {Slug}", Slug);
        await Analytics.TrackInteractionAsync("TTS.Play", Slug);
    }

    private async Task OnPause()
    {
        Logger.LogInformation("TTS pause: {Slug}", Slug);
        await Analytics.TrackInteractionAsync("TTS.Pause", Slug);
    }

    private async Task OnEnded()
    {
        Logger.LogInformation("TTS finished: {Slug}", Slug);
        await Analytics.TrackInteractionAsync("TTS.Finished", Slug);
    }
}

@inject HttpClient Http
@inject ILogger<MasterDetail> Logger
@inject IAnalyticsService Analytics
@inject IJSRuntime Js

@* ---- Confirmation dialog ---- *@
@if (_confirmAction is not null)
{
    <div class="confirm-overlay" @onclick="CancelConfirm">
        <div class="confirm-dialog" @onclick:stopPropagation="true">
            <h4>@_confirmTitle</h4>
            <p>@_confirmMessage</p>
            <div class="form-actions">
                <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="CancelConfirm">Cancel</button>
                <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" @onclick="ExecuteConfirm">@_confirmButton</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
}

<div class="md-container">
    @* ---- List pane ---- *@
    <div class="md-list">
        <div class="md-list-header">
            <h3>Products</h3>
            <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" @onclick="StartAdd" title="Add a new product">+ Add</button>
        </div>
        @if (_products is null)
        {
            <p class="md-loading"><em>Loading...</em></p>
        }
        else
        {
            <ul>
                @foreach (var p in _products)
                {
                    <li class="@(_selected?.Id == p.Id ? "md-selected" : "")"
                        @onclick="() => SelectProduct(p)">
                        <span class="md-item-name">@p.Name</span>
                        <span class="md-item-price">@p.Price.ToString("C")</span>
                    </li>
                }
            </ul>
            <div class="md-list-footer">
                <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="ConfirmReset" title="Reset to default data">
                    Reset to Defaults
                </button>
                <span class="md-count">@_products.Count items</span>
            </div>
        }
    </div>

    @* ---- Detail pane ---- *@
    <div class="md-detail">
        @if (_viewMode == ViewMode.Idle)
        {
            <div class="md-empty">
                <p>Select an item from the list, or click <strong>+ Add</strong> to create one.</p>
            </div>
        }
        else if (_viewMode == ViewMode.Viewing && _selected is not null)
        {
            <div class="md-content">
                <h3>@_selected.Name</h3>
                <dl>
                    <dt>Category</dt>
                    <dd>@_selected.Category</dd>

                    <dt>Price</dt>
                    <dd>@_selected.Price.ToString("C")</dd>

                    <dt>In Stock</dt>
                    <dd>@_selected.Stock units</dd>

                    <dt>Rating</dt>
                    <dd>
                        @for (int i = 0; i < (int)Math.Round(_selected.Rating); i++)
                        {
                            <span class="md-star">★</span>
                        }
                        (@_selected.Rating.ToString("F1"))
                    </dd>

                    <dt>Description</dt>
                    <dd>@_selected.Description</dd>
                </dl>

                <div class="form-actions" style="margin-top: 1.5rem;">
                    <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" @onclick="StartEdit">Edit</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" @onclick="ConfirmDelete">Delete</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        }
        else if (_viewMode is ViewMode.Editing or ViewMode.Adding)
        {
            <div class="md-content">
                <h3>@(_viewMode == ViewMode.Adding ? "New Product" : $"Editing: {_selected?.Name}")</h3>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="edit-name">Name</label>
                    <input id="edit-name" @bind="_editName" @bind:event="oninput" placeholder="Product name" />
                    @if (_showValidation && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_editName))
                    {
                        <div class="form-validation">Name is required.</div>
                    }
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="edit-category">Category</label>
                    <input id="edit-category" @bind="_editCategory" @bind:event="oninput" placeholder="e.g. Electronics" />
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="edit-price">Price</label>
                    <input id="edit-price" type="number" step="0.01" min="0" @bind="_editPrice" />
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="edit-stock">Stock</label>
                    <input id="edit-stock" type="number" min="0" @bind="_editStock" />
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="edit-rating">Rating (0–5)</label>
                    <input id="edit-rating" type="number" step="0.1" min="0" max="5" @bind="_editRating" />
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="edit-desc">Description</label>
                    <textarea id="edit-desc" @bind="_editDescription" @bind:event="oninput"
                              placeholder="Describe the product"></textarea>
                </div>

                <div class="form-actions">
                    <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" @onclick="SaveEdit">Save</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="CancelEdit">Cancel</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        }
    </div>
</div>

@code {
    private const string StorageKey = "observer-products-v1";

    private enum ViewMode { Idle, Viewing, Editing, Adding }

    private List<Product>? _products;
    private Product? _selected;
    private ViewMode _viewMode = ViewMode.Idle;

    // Edit form fields
    private string _editName = "";
    private string _editCategory = "";
    private decimal _editPrice;
    private int _editStock;
    private double _editRating;
    private string _editDescription = "";
    private bool _showValidation;

    // Confirmation dialog
    private string _confirmTitle = "";
    private string _confirmMessage = "";
    private string _confirmButton = "";
    private Func<Task>? _confirmAction;

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        Logger.LogInformation("Loading product data for master-detail");
        await LoadProducts();
    }

    private async Task LoadProducts()
    {
        try
        {
            // Try localStorage first
            var saved = await Js.InvokeAsync<string?>("localStorage.getItem", StorageKey);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(saved))
            {
                _products = System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Deserialize<List<Product>>(saved,
                    new System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions { PropertyNameCaseInsensitive = true });
                Logger.LogInformation("Loaded {Count} products from local storage", _products?.Count ?? 0);
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Logger.LogDebug(ex, "Could not read from local storage");
        }

        if (_products is null || _products.Count == 0)
        {
            try
            {
                var loaded = await Http.GetFromJsonAsync<Product[]>("sample-data/products.json");
                _products = loaded?.ToList() ?? [];
                Logger.LogInformation("Loaded {Count} products from JSON", _products.Count);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Logger.LogError(ex, "Failed to load products");
                _products = [];
            }
        }
    }

    private async Task SaveProducts()
    {
        if (_products is null) return;
        try
        {
            var json = System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Serialize(_products,
                new System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions { PropertyNamingPolicy = System.Text.Json.JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase });
            await Js.InvokeVoidAsync("localStorage.setItem", StorageKey, json);
            Logger.LogInformation("Saved {Count} products to local storage", _products.Count);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Logger.LogWarning(ex, "Could not save to local storage");
        }
    }

    private async Task SelectProduct(Product product)
    {
        if (_viewMode is ViewMode.Editing or ViewMode.Adding) return;
        _selected = product;
        _viewMode = ViewMode.Viewing;
        Logger.LogInformation("Selected product: {Name}", product.Name);
        await Analytics.TrackInteractionAsync("ProductSelect", product.Name);
    }

    // ---- CRUD operations ----

    private void StartAdd()
    {
        _selected = null;
        _editName = "";
        _editCategory = "";
        _editPrice = 0;
        _editStock = 0;
        _editRating = 0;
        _editDescription = "";
        _showValidation = false;
        _viewMode = ViewMode.Adding;
    }

    private void StartEdit()
    {
        if (_selected is null) return;
        _editName = _selected.Name;
        _editCategory = _selected.Category;
        _editPrice = _selected.Price;
        _editStock = _selected.Stock;
        _editRating = _selected.Rating;
        _editDescription = _selected.Description;
        _showValidation = false;
        _viewMode = ViewMode.Editing;
    }

    private async Task SaveEdit()
    {
        _showValidation = true;
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_editName)) return;

        if (_viewMode == ViewMode.Adding)
        {
            var newProduct = new Product
            {
                Id = Guid.NewGuid(),
                Name = _editName.Trim(),
                Category = _editCategory.Trim(),
                Price = _editPrice,
                Stock = _editStock,
                Rating = Math.Clamp(_editRating, 0, 5),
                Description = _editDescription.Trim()
            };
            _products!.Add(newProduct);
            _selected = newProduct;
            Logger.LogInformation("Added product: {Name}", newProduct.Name);
            await Analytics.TrackInteractionAsync("ProductAdd", newProduct.Name);
        }
        else if (_viewMode == ViewMode.Editing && _selected is not null)
        {
            var idx = _products!.FindIndex(p => p.Id == _selected.Id);
            if (idx >= 0)
            {
                var updated = _selected with
                {
                    Name = _editName.Trim(),
                    Category = _editCategory.Trim(),
                    Price = _editPrice,
                    Stock = _editStock,
                    Rating = Math.Clamp(_editRating, 0, 5),
                    Description = _editDescription.Trim()
                };
                _products[idx] = updated;
                _selected = updated;
                Logger.LogInformation("Updated product: {Name}", updated.Name);
                await Analytics.TrackInteractionAsync("ProductUpdate", updated.Name);
            }
        }

        await SaveProducts();
        _viewMode = ViewMode.Viewing;
        _showValidation = false;
    }

    private void CancelEdit()
    {
        _viewMode = _selected is not null ? ViewMode.Viewing : ViewMode.Idle;
        _showValidation = false;
    }

    private void ConfirmDelete()
    {
        if (_selected is null) return;
        _confirmTitle = "Delete Product";
        _confirmMessage = $"Are you sure you want to delete \"{_selected.Name}\"? This cannot be undone.";
        _confirmButton = "Delete";
        _confirmAction = ExecuteDelete;
    }

    private async Task ExecuteDelete()
    {
        if (_selected is not null && _products is not null)
        {
            var name = _selected.Name;
            _products.RemoveAll(p => p.Id == _selected.Id);
            _selected = null;
            _viewMode = ViewMode.Idle;
            await SaveProducts();
            Logger.LogInformation("Deleted product: {Name}", name);
            await Analytics.TrackInteractionAsync("ProductDelete", name);
        }
        _confirmAction = null;
    }

    private void ConfirmReset()
    {
        _confirmTitle = "Reset to Defaults";
        _confirmMessage = "This will discard all your changes and restore the original product data. Continue?";
        _confirmButton = "Reset";
        _confirmAction = ExecuteReset;
    }

    private async Task ExecuteReset()
    {
        try
        {
            await Js.InvokeVoidAsync("localStorage.removeItem", StorageKey);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Logger.LogDebug(ex, "Could not clear local storage");
        }

        _products = null;
        _selected = null;
        _viewMode = ViewMode.Idle;
        await LoadProducts();
        Logger.LogInformation("Reset products to defaults");
        await Analytics.TrackInteractionAsync("ProductReset", "");
        _confirmAction = null;
    }

    private void CancelConfirm()
    {
        _confirmAction = null;
    }

    private async Task ExecuteConfirm()
    {
        if (_confirmAction is not null)
        {
            await _confirmAction();
        }
    }
}

<div class="progress-meter">
    <div class="progress-meter-track"
         role="progressbar"
         aria-valuemin="0"
         aria-valuemax="100"
         aria-valuenow="@ClampedPercent"
         aria-label="@(Label ?? "Progress")">
        <div class="progress-meter-fill" style="width: @(ClampedPercent)%"></div>
    </div>
    @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Label))
    {
        <span class="progress-meter-label">@Label</span>
    }
</div>

@code {
    [Parameter] public int Percent { get; set; }
    [Parameter] public string? Label { get; set; }

    private int ClampedPercent => Math.Clamp(Percent, 0, 100);
}

<fieldset class="quiz-question @StateClass" id="@ElementId">
    <legend class="quiz-legend">
        <span class="quiz-number">Question @Number</span>
        <span class="quiz-mode">@(Question.MultipleAnswers ? "Select all that apply" : "Select one answer")</span>
    </legend>

    <div class="quiz-prompt">@((MarkupString)Question.PromptHtml)</div>

    <ul class="quiz-options">
        @foreach (var option in Question.Options)
        {
            var inputId = $"{ElementId}-{option.Id}";
            <li class="@OptionClass(option)">
                <input type="@(Question.MultipleAnswers ? "checkbox" : "radio")"
                       id="@inputId"
                       name="@ElementId"
                       value="@option.Id"
                       checked="@_selected.Contains(option.Id)"
                       disabled="@IsLocked"
                       @onchange="e => OnOptionChanged(option.Id, e)" />
                <label for="@inputId">@((MarkupString)option.TextHtml)</label>
            </li>
        }
    </ul>

    <div class="quiz-actions">
        @if (_result is null)
        {
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm quiz-submit" disabled="@(_selected.Count == 0)" @onclick="SubmitAsync">
                Check answer
            </button>
        }
        else if (!_result.IsCorrect)
        {
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline btn-sm quiz-retry" @onclick="Retry">Try again</button>
            if (!_revealed)
            {
                <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline btn-sm quiz-reveal" @onclick="Reveal">Show answer</button>
            }
        }
        @if (_attempts > 0)
        {
            <span class="quiz-attempts">@_attempts @(_attempts == 1 ? "attempt" : "attempts")</span>
        }
    </div>

    @if (_result is not null)
    {
        <div class="quiz-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite">
            @if (_result.IsCorrect)
            {
                <strong class="quiz-verdict">Correct!</strong>
            }
            else
            {
                <strong class="quiz-verdict">Not quite.</strong>
                if (Question.MultipleAnswers && !_revealed)
                {
                    <span> Select every correct answer and nothing else.</span>
                }
            }
            @if (ShowSolution && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(Question.ExplanationHtml))
            {
                <div class="quiz-explanation">@((MarkupString)Question.ExplanationHtml)</div>
            }
        </div>
    }
</fieldset>

@code {
    [Parameter, EditorRequired] public QuizQuestion Question { get; set; } = null!;
    [Parameter] public int Number { get; set; } = 1;
    [Parameter] public QuestionProgress? InitialProgress { get; set; }
    [Parameter] public EventCallback<QuizAnswer> OnAnswered { get; set; }

    private readonly HashSet<string> _selected = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    private QuizResult? _result;
    private bool _revealed;
    private int _attempts;

    private string ElementId => $"quiz-{Question.Id}";
    private bool IsLocked => _result is not null;
    private bool ShowSolution => _result is { IsCorrect: true } || _revealed;

    private string StateClass => _result switch
    {
        null => "",
        { IsCorrect: true } => "is-correct",
        _ => "is-incorrect"
    };

    protected override void OnInitialized()
    {
        if (InitialProgress is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        _attempts = InitialProgress.Attempts;
        if (!InitialProgress.LastAttemptCorrect)
        {
            return;
        }

        var restored = QuizEvaluator.Evaluate(Question, InitialProgress.LastSelection);
        if (restored.IsCorrect)
        {
            _selected.UnionWith(restored.Selected);
            _result = restored;
        }
    }

    private string OptionClass(QuizOption option)
    {
        var classes = new List<string> { "quiz-option" };
        if (_selected.Contains(option.Id))
        {
            classes.Add("is-selected");
        }

        if (_result is not null)
        {
            if (ShowSolution && option.Correct)
            {
                classes.Add("is-answer");
            }
            if (_result.IsWronglySelected(option.Id))
            {
                classes.Add("is-wrong");
            }
        }

        return string.Join(' ', classes);
    }

    private void OnOptionChanged(string optionId, ChangeEventArgs e)
    {
        if (IsLocked)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (!Question.MultipleAnswers)
        {
            _selected.Clear();
            _selected.Add(optionId);
            return;
        }

        var isChecked = e.Value switch
        {
            bool b => b,
            string s => bool.TryParse(s, out var parsed) && parsed,
            _ => false
        };

        if (isChecked)
        {
            _selected.Add(optionId);
        }
        else
        {
            _selected.Remove(optionId);
        }
    }

    private async Task SubmitAsync()
    {
        if (_selected.Count == 0 || _result is not null)
        {
            return;
        }

        _result = QuizEvaluator.Evaluate(Question, _selected);
        _attempts++;
        await OnAnswered.InvokeAsync(new QuizAnswer(Question, _selected.ToArray(), _result));
    }

    private void Retry()
    {
        _result = null;
        _revealed = false;
        _selected.Clear();
    }

    private void Reveal() => _revealed = true;
}

.quiz-question {

    border-radius: var(--radius);

    margin: 0 0 1.25rem;
    min-width: 0;

    flex-wrap: wrap;

.quiz-prompt ::deep code,
.quiz-explanation ::deep code {
    font-family: var(--font-mono);
    font-size: 0.875em;
    background: var(--color-inline-code-bg);
    border: 1px solid var(--color-inline-code-border);
    border-radius: 4px;

}

.quiz-actions {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    align-items: center;

    margin-top: 1rem;
}

.quiz-submit:disabled {
    opacity: 0.5;
    cursor: not-allowed;
}

.quiz-attempts {
    font-size: 0.8rem;
    color: var(--color-muted);
    margin-left: auto;
}

.quiz-feedback {
    margin-top: 0.75rem;
    padding: 0.75rem 1rem;
    border-radius: var(--radius);

FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Components/Reactions.razor
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
@inject IAnalyticsService Analytics

@if (_reactions is not null)
{
    <div class="reactions-bar">
        @foreach (var (emoji, type) in ReactionTypes)
        {
            var count = _reactions.GetValueOrDefault(type, 0);
            <button class="reaction-btn @(_userReacted == type ? "reacted" : "")"
                    @onclick="() => React(type)"
                    title="@type">
                <span class="reaction-emoji">@emoji</span>
                @if (count > 0)
                {
                    <span class="reaction-count">@count</span>
                }
            </button>
        }
    </div>
}

@code {
    [Parameter] public string Slug { get; set; } = "";

    private static readonly (string Emoji, string Type)[] ReactionTypes =
    [
        ("👍", "like"),
        ("❤️", "love"),
        ("🎉", "celebrate"),
        ("🤔", "thinking")
    ];

    private Dictionary<string, int>? _reactions;
    private string? _userReacted;

    protected override async Task OnParametersSetAsync()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Slug)) return;

        _reactions = await Analytics.GetReactionsAsync(Slug);
        // If backend returned null, reactions stays null and the UI is hidden
    }

    private async Task React(string type)
    {
        if (_reactions is null || _userReacted == type) return;

        _userReacted = type;
        await Analytics.AddReactionAsync(Slug, type);

        // Optimistic update
        _reactions[type] = _reactions.GetValueOrDefault(type, 0) + 1;
        await Analytics.TrackInteractionAsync("Reaction", $"{Slug}: {type}");
    }
}

FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Components/ResponsiveTable.razor
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
@inject HttpClient Http
@inject ILogger<ResponsiveTable> Logger
@inject IAnalyticsService Analytics

@if (_products is null)
{
    <p><em>Loading data...</em></p>
}
else
{
    <div class="rt-controls">
        <input type="text"
               placeholder="Filter by name..."
               @bind="_filterText"
               @bind:event="oninput"
               class="rt-filter-input" />
    </div>

    <div class="rt-table-responsive">
        <table class="rt-data-table">
            <thead>
                <tr>
                    <th class="rt-sortable" @onclick='() => Sort("Name")'>
                        Name @SortIndicator("Name")
                    </th>
                    <th class="rt-sortable" @onclick='() => Sort("Category")'>
                        Category @SortIndicator("Category")
                    </th>
                    <th class="rt-sortable rt-numeric" @onclick='() => Sort("Price")'>
                        Price @SortIndicator("Price")
                    </th>
                    <th class="rt-sortable rt-numeric" @onclick='() => Sort("Stock")'>
                        Stock @SortIndicator("Stock")
                    </th>
                    <th>Rating</th>
                </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
                @foreach (var p in FilteredAndSorted)
                {
                    <tr>
                        <td data-label="Name">@p.Name</td>
                        <td data-label="Category">@p.Category</td>
                        <td data-label="Price" class="rt-numeric">@p.Price.ToString("C")</td>
                        <td data-label="Stock" class="rt-numeric">@p.Stock</td>
                        <td data-label="Rating">
                            @for (int i = 0; i < (int)Math.Round(p.Rating); i++)
                            {
                                <span class="rt-star">★</span>
                            }
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                }
            </tbody>
        </table>
    </div>

    <p class="rt-info">
        Showing @FilteredAndSorted.Count() of @_products.Length items
    </p>
}

@code {
    private Product[]? _products;
    private string _filterText = "";
    private string _sortColumn = "Name";
    private bool _sortAscending = true;

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        Logger.LogInformation("Loading product data for responsive table");
        try
        {
            _products = await Http.GetFromJsonAsync<Product[]>("sample-data/products.json");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Logger.LogError(ex, "Failed to load products");
            _products = [];
        }
    }

    private IEnumerable<Product> FilteredAndSorted
    {
        get
        {
            if (_products is null) return [];

            var filtered = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_filterText)
                ? _products.AsEnumerable()
                : _products.Where(p =>
                    p.Name.Contains(_filterText, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

            return _sortColumn switch
            {
                "Name" => _sortAscending ? filtered.OrderBy(p => p.Name) : filtered.OrderByDescending(p => p.Name),
                "Category" => _sortAscending ? filtered.OrderBy(p => p.Category) : filtered.OrderByDescending(p => p.Category),
                "Price" => _sortAscending ? filtered.OrderBy(p => p.Price) : filtered.OrderByDescending(p => p.Price),
                "Stock" => _sortAscending ? filtered.OrderBy(p => p.Stock) : filtered.OrderByDescending(p => p.Stock),
                _ => filtered
            };
        }
    }

    private async Task Sort(string column)
    {
        if (_sortColumn == column)
        {
            _sortAscending = !_sortAscending;
        }
        else
        {
            _sortColumn = column;
            _sortAscending = true;
        }
        Logger.LogInformation("Sorted by {Column} {Direction}", column, _sortAscending ? "asc" : "desc");
        await Analytics.TrackInteractionAsync("TableSort", $"{column} {(_sortAscending ? "asc" : "desc")}");
    }

    private string SortIndicator(string column)
    {
        if (_sortColumn != column) return "";
        return _sortAscending ? "▲" : "▼";
    }
}

@code {
    [Parameter, EditorRequired] public string Html { get; set; } = "";

    private string _processedHtml = "";
    private string? _sourceHtml;

    protected override void OnParametersSet()
    {
        if (string.Equals(_sourceHtml, Html, StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            return;
        }

        _sourceHtml = Html;
        _processedHtml = HtmlPostProcessor.Process(Html);
    }
}

FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Components/SubchapterQuiz.razor
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
@inject IReadingProgressService ProgressService
@inject IBookAnalytics BookAnalytics

@if (Content.Questions.Length > 0)
{
    <section class="subchapter-quiz" aria-labelledby="quiz-heading">
        <header class="quiz-header">
            <h2 id="quiz-heading">Check your understanding</h2>
            <span class="quiz-score">@CorrectCount of @Content.Questions.Length answered correctly</span>
        </header>

        @for (var i = 0; i < Content.Questions.Length; i++)
        {
            var question = Content.Questions[i];
            var number = i + 1;
            <QuizQuestionCard @key="question.Id"
                              Question="question"
                              Number="number"
                              InitialProgress="GetQuestionProgress(question.Id)"
                              OnAnswered="HandleAnsweredAsync" />
        }

        @if (CorrectCount == Content.Questions.Length)
        {
            <p class="quiz-complete" role="status">Nice work, you have completed this section.</p>
        }
    </section>
}

@code {
    [Parameter, EditorRequired] public string BookSlug { get; set; } = "";
    [Parameter, EditorRequired] public SubchapterContent Content { get; set; } = null!;
    [Parameter] public SubchapterProgress? Progress { get; set; }
    [Parameter] public EventCallback<BookProgress> OnProgressChanged { get; set; }

    private int CorrectCount => Progress is { } progress
        ? Content.Questions.Count(q => progress.Questions.TryGetValue(q.Id, out var p) && p.AnsweredCorrectly)
        : 0;

    private QuestionProgress? GetQuestionProgress(string questionId) =>
        Progress?.Questions.GetValueOrDefault(questionId);

    private async Task HandleAnsweredAsync(QuizAnswer answer)
    {
        var progress = await ProgressService.RecordAnswerAsync(
            BookSlug,
            Content.ChapterSlug,
            Content.Slug,
            answer.Question.Id,
            answer.SelectedOptionIds,
            answer.Result.IsCorrect);

        await OnProgressChanged.InvokeAsync(progress);
        await BookAnalytics.RecordQuizAnswerAsync(BookSlug, Content.ChapterSlug, Content.Slug, answer.Question.Id, answer.Result.IsCorrect);
    }
}

@inherits LayoutComponentBase

<div class="page" data-theme="@_theme.Id">

        <div class="container top-bar-inner">
            <a class="brand" href="/">
                <strong>Terlingua</strong> Magazine
            </a>
            <NavMenu CurrentTheme="_theme" ThemeChanged="SetThemeAsync" />
        </div>
    </header>

    <main class="container main-content">
        @Body
    </main>

    <footer class="site-footer">
        <div class="container">
            <p>
                &copy; @DateTime.UtcNow.Year Terlingua Magazine &mdash; Built with
                <a href="https://dotnet.microsoft.com/apps/aspnet/web-apps/blazor" target="_blank"
                   rel="noopener noreferrer">Blazor WebAssembly</a>
                on .NET 11.
            </p>
            <p>
                <a href="/feed.xml">RSS Feed</a>
                &middot;
                <a href="https://github.com/terlingua/terlingua.github.io"
                   target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Source on GitHub</a>
            </p>
        </div>
    </footer>
</div>

@code {
    private Theme _theme = Theme.Default;

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        try
        {
            _theme = Theme.FromId(await Storage.GetAsync(Theme.StorageKey));
        }
        catch (BrowserStorageException ex)
        {
            Logger.LogDebug(ex, "Could not read the saved theme");
        }
    }

    private async Task SetThemeAsync(Theme theme)
    {
        _theme = theme;
        try
        {
            await Storage.SetAsync(Theme.StorageKey, theme.Id);
        }
        catch (BrowserStorageException ex)
        {
            Logger.LogDebug(ex, "Could not save the theme");
        }
    }
}

@code {
    [Parameter, EditorRequired] public Theme CurrentTheme { get; set; } = Theme.Default;
    [Parameter] public EventCallback<Theme> ThemeChanged { get; set; }

    private bool _isOpen;

    protected override void OnInitialized()
    {
        Nav.LocationChanged += OnLocationChanged;
    }

    private void ToggleMenu() => _isOpen = !_isOpen;

    private void CloseMenu() => _isOpen = false;

    private void OnLocationChanged(object? sender, LocationChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (_isOpen)
        {
            _isOpen = false;
            InvokeAsync(StateHasChanged);
        }
    }

    private Task OnThemeChanged(ChangeEventArgs e) =>
        ThemeChanged.InvokeAsync(Theme.FromId(e.Value?.ToString()));

    public void Dispose()
    {
        Nav.LocationChanged -= OnLocationChanged;
    }
}

namespace ObserverMagazine.Web.Models;

public sealed class BookProgress
{
    public const int CurrentSchemaVersion = 1;

    public int SchemaVersion { get; set; } = CurrentSchemaVersion;
    public string BookSlug { get; set; } = "";
    public DateTimeOffset? StartedAt { get; set; }
    public DateTimeOffset? UpdatedAt { get; set; }
    public string? LastChapterSlug { get; set; }
    public string? LastSubchapterSlug { get; set; }
    public Dictionary<string, SubchapterProgress> Subchapters { get; set; } = [with(StringComparer.Ordinal)];

    public bool IsStarted => StartedAt is not null;

    public static string SubchapterKey(string chapterSlug, string subchapterSlug) => $"{chapterSlug}/{subchapterSlug}";
}

public sealed class SubchapterProgress
{
    public DateTimeOffset? FirstVisitedAt { get; set; }
    public DateTimeOffset? LastVisitedAt { get; set; }
    public Dictionary<string, QuestionProgress> Questions { get; set; } = [with(StringComparer.Ordinal)];
}

public sealed class QuestionProgress
{
    public int Attempts { get; set; }
    public bool AnsweredCorrectly { get; set; }
    public bool LastAttemptCorrect { get; set; }
    public string[] LastSelection { get; set; } = [];
    public DateTimeOffset? LastAttemptAt { get; set; }
}

public enum SubchapterStatus
{
    NotStarted,
    InProgress,
    Completed
}

public sealed record BookCompletion(int CompletedSubchapters, int TotalSubchapters, int CorrectQuestions, int TotalQuestions)
{
    public static readonly BookCompletion Empty = new(0, 0, 0, 0);

    public int Percent => TotalSubchapters == 0 ? 0 : CompletedSubchapters * 100 / TotalSubchapters;
    public bool IsComplete => TotalSubchapters > 0 && CompletedSubchapters == TotalSubchapters;
}

public sealed record VisitOutcome(BookProgress Progress, bool IsFirstVisitToBook, bool IsFirstVisitToSubchapter);

FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Models/BookToc.cs

namespace ObserverMagazine.Web.Models;

public sealed record BookToc
{
    public required string Slug { get; init; }
    public required string Title { get; init; }
    public DateTime Date { get; init; }
    public DateTime? Updated { get; init; }
    public string Author { get; init; } = "";
    public string AuthorName { get; init; } = "";
    public string Summary { get; init; } = "";
    public string[] Tags { get; init; } = [];
    public string? Image { get; init; }
    public ChapterToc[] Chapters { get; init; } = [];

    public int SubchapterCount => Chapters.Sum(c => c.Subchapters.Length);
    public int QuestionCount => Chapters.Sum(c => c.Subchapters.Sum(s => s.QuestionIds.Length));
}

public sealed record ChapterToc
{
    public int Number { get; init; }
    public required string Slug { get; init; }
    public required string Title { get; init; }
    public string Summary { get; init; } = "";
    public SubchapterToc[] Subchapters { get; init; } = [];
}

public sealed record SubchapterToc
{
    public int Number { get; init; }
    public required string Slug { get; init; }
    public required string Title { get; init; }
    public int ReadingTimeMinutes { get; init; }
    public string[] QuestionIds { get; init; } = [];
}

public sealed record SubchapterLocation(ChapterToc Chapter, SubchapterToc Subchapter)
{
    public string Label => $"{Chapter.Number}.{Subchapter.Number}";
    public string ProgressKey => BookProgress.SubchapterKey(Chapter.Slug, Subchapter.Slug);
    public string Href(string bookSlug) => $"books/{bookSlug}/{Chapter.Slug}/{Subchapter.Slug}";
}

public sealed record SubchapterNeighbors(SubchapterLocation? Previous, SubchapterLocation? Next);

namespace ObserverMagazine.Web.Models;

public sealed record SubchapterContent
{
    public required string BookSlug { get; init; }
    public required string ChapterSlug { get; init; }
    public required string Slug { get; init; }
    public int ChapterNumber { get; init; }
    public int Number { get; init; }
    public required string Title { get; init; }
    public int ReadingTimeMinutes { get; init; }
    public string Html { get; init; } = "";
    public QuizQuestion[] Questions { get; init; } = [];
}

public sealed record QuizQuestion
{
    public required string Id { get; init; }
    public string PromptHtml { get; init; } = "";
    public bool MultipleAnswers { get; init; }
    public string ExplanationHtml { get; init; } = "";
    public QuizOption[] Options { get; init; } = [];
}

{
    public required string Id { get; init; }
    public string TextHtml { get; init; } = "";
    public bool Correct { get; init; }
}

public sealed record QuizResult(bool IsCorrect, IReadOnlySet<string> Selected, IReadOnlySet<string> Correct)
{
    public bool IsWronglySelected(string optionId) => Selected.Contains(optionId) && !Correct.Contains(optionId);
}

public sealed record QuizAnswer(QuizQuestion Question, IReadOnlyCollection<string> SelectedOptionIds, QuizResult Result);

FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Models/Theme.cs
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
namespace ObserverMagazine.Web.Models;

public sealed record Theme(string Id, string Label)
{
    public const string StorageKey = "observer-theme";

    public static readonly Theme Default = new("system", "System");

    public static readonly IReadOnlyList<Theme> All =
    [
        Default,
        new("light", "Light"),
        new("dark", "Dark"),
        new("sepia", "Sepia"),
        new("solarized-light", "Solarized Light"),
        new("solarized-dark", "Solarized Dark"),
        new("high-contrast", "High Contrast")
    ];

    public static Theme FromId(string? id) =>
        All.FirstOrDefault(t => string.Equals(t.Id, id, StringComparison.Ordinal)) ?? Default;
}

FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Pages/BlogPost.razor
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
@page "/blog/{Slug}"
@inject IBlogService BlogService
@inject ILogger<BlogPost> Logger
@inject IAnalyticsService Analytics

<PageTitle>@(_metadata?.Title ?? "Post") — Terlingua Magazine</PageTitle>

@if (_loading)
{
    <p><em>Loading...</em></p>
}
else if (_metadata is null)
{
    <h1>Post Not Found</h1>
    <p>Sorry, we couldn't find that post. <a href="blog">Back to Blog</a></p>
}
else
{
    <article class="blog-post">
        <header>
            <h1>@_metadata.Title</h1>
            <div class="blog-meta">
                <time datetime="@_metadata.Date.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd")">
                    @_metadata.Date.ToString("MMMM d, yyyy")
                </time>
                @if (_metadata.Updated.HasValue)
                {
                    <span> · Updated @_metadata.Updated.Value.ToString("MMMM d, yyyy")</span>
                }
                @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_metadata.AuthorName))
                {
                    <span> · @_metadata.AuthorName</span>
                }
                @if (_metadata.ReadingTimeMinutes > 0)
                {
                    <span> · @_metadata.ReadingTimeMinutes min read</span>
                }
                @if (_viewCount.HasValue)
                {
                    <span> · @_viewCount views</span>
                }
            </div>
            @if (_metadata.Tags is { Length: > 0 })
            {
                <div class="tag-list">
                    @foreach (var tag in _metadata.Tags)
                    {
                        <a class="tag" href="blog/tag/@Uri.EscapeDataString(tag)">@tag</a>
                    }
                </div>
            }
        </header>

        <BlogTtsPlayer Slug="@Slug" />

        <RichContent Html="@_htmlContent" />

        <Reactions Slug="@Slug" />

        <AuthorCard Author="_authorProfile" />

        <footer class="blog-post-footer">
            <a href="blog">&larr; Back to Blog</a>
        </footer>
    </article>
}

@code {
    [Parameter] public string Slug { get; set; } = "";

    private BlogPostMetadata? _metadata;
    private string _htmlContent = "";
    private bool _loading = true;
    private AuthorProfile? _authorProfile;
    private int? _viewCount;

    protected override async Task OnParametersSetAsync()
    {
        _loading = true;
        Logger.LogInformation("Loading blog post: {Slug}", Slug);

        try
        {
            _metadata = await BlogService.GetPostMetadataAsync(Slug);
            if (_metadata is not null)
            {
                _htmlContent = await BlogService.GetPostHtmlAsync(Slug);

                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_metadata.Author))
                {
                    _authorProfile = await BlogService.GetAuthorAsync(_metadata.Author);
                }

                await Analytics.TrackPageViewAsync("BlogPost", $"{_metadata.Title} ({Slug})");
                await Analytics.IncrementViewAsync(Slug);
                _viewCount = await Analytics.GetViewCountAsync(Slug);
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Logger.LogError(ex, "Failed to load post {Slug}", Slug);
            _metadata = null;
        }
        finally
        {
            _loading = false;
        }
    }
}

FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Pages/Blog.razor

@page "/blog"
@page "/blog/tag/{Tag}"
@inject IBlogService BlogService
@inject ILogger<Blog> Logger
@inject IAnalyticsService Analytics
@inject NavigationManager Nav

<PageTitle>@(string.IsNullOrEmpty(Tag) ? "Blog" : $"Posts tagged \"{Tag}\"") — Terlingua Magazine</PageTitle>

<h1>Blog</h1>

@if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Tag))
{
    <div class="tag-filter-bar">
        <span>Filtering by tag:</span>
        <span class="tag">@Tag</span>
        <button class="tag-filter-clear" @onclick="ClearFilter">Clear filter</button>
    </div>
}

@if (_posts is null)
{
    <p><em>Loading posts...</em></p>
}
else if (_filteredPosts.Length == 0)
{
    <p>No posts found. <a href="blog">View all posts</a></p>
}
else
{
    <div class="blog-list">
        @foreach (var post in _filteredPosts)
        {
            <article class="blog-card">
                @if (post.Featured)
                {
                    <span class="featured-badge">Featured</span>
                }
                <h2><a href="blog/@post.Slug">@post.Title</a></h2>
                <div class="blog-meta">
                    <time datetime="@post.Date.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd")">
                        @post.Date.ToString("MMMM d, yyyy")
                    </time>
                    @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(post.AuthorName))
                    {
                        <span> · @post.AuthorName</span>
                    }
                    @if (post.ReadingTimeMinutes > 0)
                    {
                        <span> · @post.ReadingTimeMinutes min read</span>
                    }
                    @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(post.Series))
                    {
                        <span> · Series: @post.Series</span>
                    }
                </div>
                <p>@post.Summary</p>
                @if (post.Tags is { Length: > 0 })
                {
                    <div class="tag-list">
                        @foreach (var tag in post.Tags)
                        {
                            <a class="tag" href="blog/tag/@Uri.EscapeDataString(tag)">@tag</a>
                        }
                    </div>
                }
            </article>
        }
    </div>
}

@code {
    [Parameter] public string? Tag { get; set; }

    private BlogPostMetadata[]? _posts;
    private BlogPostMetadata[] _filteredPosts = [];

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        Logger.LogInformation("Loading blog index");
        try
        {
            _posts = await BlogService.GetPostsAsync();
            await Analytics.TrackPageViewAsync("Blog", $"{_posts?.Length ?? 0} posts");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Logger.LogError(ex, "Failed to load blog index");
            _posts = [];
        }
        ApplyFilter();
    }

    protected override void OnParametersSet()
    {
        ApplyFilter();
    }

    private void ApplyFilter()
    {
        if (_posts is null)
        {
            _filteredPosts = [];
            return;
        }

        _filteredPosts = string.IsNullOrEmpty(Tag)
            ? _posts
            : _posts.Where(p =>
                p.Tags.Any(t => string.Equals(t, Tag, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
              .ToArray();
    }

    private void ClearFilter()
    {
        Nav.NavigateTo("blog");
    }
}

FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Pages/BookDetail.razor
