@page "/books/{BookSlug}"
@inject IBookService BookService
@inject IBlogService BlogService
@inject IReadingProgressService ProgressService
@inject IBookAnalytics BookAnalytics
@inject IAnalyticsService Analytics
@inject ILogger<BookDetail> Logger

<PageTitle>@(_toc?.Title ?? "Book") — Terlingua Magazine</PageTitle>

@if (_loading)
{
    <p><em>Loading...</em></p>
}
else if (_toc is null)
{
    <h1>Book Not Found</h1>
    <p>Sorry, we couldn't find that book. <a href="books">Back to Books</a></p>
}
else
{
    <article class="book-detail">
        <header class="book-header">
            <h1>@_toc.Title</h1>
            <div class="blog-meta">
                @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_toc.AuthorName))
                {
                    <span>@_toc.AuthorName · </span>
                }
                <span>@_toc.Chapters.Length @(_toc.Chapters.Length == 1 ? "chapter" : "chapters")</span>
                <span> · @_toc.SubchapterCount sections</span>
                @if (_toc.QuestionCount > 0)
                {
                    <span> · @_toc.QuestionCount questions</span>
                }
                @if (_readerCount.HasValue)
                {
                    <span> · @_readerCount @(_readerCount == 1 ? "reader" : "readers")</span>
                }
            </div>

            @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_toc.Summary))
            {
                <p class="book-summary">@_toc.Summary</p>
            }

            @if (_toc.Tags is { Length: > 0 })
            {
                <div class="tag-list">
                    @foreach (var tag in _toc.Tags)
                    {
                        <span class="tag">@tag</span>
                    }
                </div>
            }

            <div class="book-progress-panel">
                <ProgressMeter Percent="@_completion.Percent" Label="@ProgressLabel" />
                <div class="book-actions">
                    @if (_continue is not null)
                    {
                        <a class="btn btn-primary" href="@_continue.Href(_toc.Slug)">
                            @(_progress?.IsStarted == true ? "Continue" : "Start reading") · @_continue.Label
                        </a>
                    }
                    @if (_progress?.IsStarted == true)
                    {
                        if (_confirmReset)
                        {
                            <span class="reset-confirm">
                                Reset all progress for this book?
                                <button type="button" class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" @onclick="ResetAsync">Reset</button>
                                <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="() => _confirmReset = false">Cancel</button>
                            </span>
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="() => _confirmReset = true">Reset progress</button>
                        }
                    }
                </div>
                @if (!_isPersistent)
                {
                    <p class="storage-note">This browser can't store progress, so it will reset when you leave the site.</p>
                }
            </div>
        </header>

        <section class="book-toc" aria-labelledby="toc-heading">
            <h2 id="toc-heading">Contents</h2>
            <ol class="chapter-list">
                @foreach (var chapter in _toc.Chapters)
                {
                    <li class="chapter-item">
                        <h3 class="chapter-title">
                            <span class="chapter-number">Chapter @chapter.Number</span>
                            @chapter.Title
                        </h3>
                        @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(chapter.Summary))
                        {
                            <p class="chapter-summary">@chapter.Summary</p>
                        }
                        <ol class="subchapter-list">
                            @foreach (var subchapter in chapter.Subchapters)
                            {
                                var location = new SubchapterLocation(chapter, subchapter);
                                var status = BookProgressCalculator.GetStatus(location, _progress);
                                var subProgress = _progress?.Subchapters.GetValueOrDefault(location.ProgressKey);
                                <li class="@($"subchapter-item {StatusCss(status)}")">
                                    <span class="status-icon" role="img" aria-label="@StatusText(status)" title="@StatusText(status)">@StatusGlyph(status)</span>
                                    <a href="@location.Href(_toc.Slug)">
                                        <span class="subchapter-number">@location.Label</span>
                                        @subchapter.Title
                                    </a>
                                    <span class="subchapter-meta">
                                        @subchapter.ReadingTimeMinutes min
                                        @if (subchapter.QuestionIds.Length > 0)
                                        {
                                            <span> · @(BookProgressCalculator.CountCorrect(subchapter, subProgress))/@(subchapter.QuestionIds.Length) correct</span>
                                        }
                                    </span>
                                </li>
                            }
                        </ol>
                    </li>
                }
            </ol>
        </section>

        <AuthorCard Author="_author" />

        <footer class="book-footer">
            <a href="books">&larr; All books</a>
        </footer>
    </article>
}

@code {
    [Parameter] public string BookSlug { get; set; } = "";

    private bool _loading = true;
    private BookToc? _toc;
    private BookProgress? _progress;
    private BookCompletion _completion = BookCompletion.Empty;
    private SubchapterLocation? _continue;
    private AuthorProfile? _author;
    private int? _readerCount;
    private bool _isPersistent = true;
    private bool _confirmReset;
    private string? _loadedSlug;

    private string ProgressLabel =>
        _completion.TotalQuestions > 0
            ? $"{_completion.Percent}% complete · {_completion.CompletedSubchapters} of {_completion.TotalSubchapters} sections · {_completion.CorrectQuestions} of {_completion.TotalQuestions} questions correct"
            : $"{_completion.Percent}% complete · {_completion.CompletedSubchapters} of {_completion.TotalSubchapters} sections";

    protected override async Task OnParametersSetAsync()
    {
        if (string.Equals(_loadedSlug, BookSlug, StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            return;
        }

        _loadedSlug = BookSlug;
        _loading = true;
        _readerCount = null;
        _confirmReset = false;

        try
        {
            _toc = await BookService.GetTocAsync(BookSlug);
            if (_toc is not null)
            {
                ApplyProgress(await ProgressService.GetAsync(_toc.Slug));
                _isPersistent = await ProgressService.IsPersistentAsync();
                _author = string.IsNullOrEmpty(_toc.Author) ? null : await BlogService.GetAuthorAsync(_toc.Author);
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Logger.LogError(ex, "Failed to load book {Slug}", BookSlug);
            _toc = null;
        }
        finally
        {
            _loading = false;
        }

        if (_toc is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        StateHasChanged();
        var slug = _toc.Slug;
        await Analytics.TrackPageViewAsync("Book", slug);
        var count = await BookAnalytics.GetReaderCountAsync(slug);
        if (string.Equals(_toc?.Slug, slug, StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            _readerCount = count;
        }
    }

    private void ApplyProgress(BookProgress progress)
    {
        if (_toc is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        _progress = progress;
        _completion = BookProgressCalculator.GetCompletion(_toc, progress);
        _continue = BookProgressCalculator.GetContinueLocation(_toc, progress);
    }

    private async Task ResetAsync()
    {
        if (_toc is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        ApplyProgress(await ProgressService.ResetAsync(_toc.Slug));
        _confirmReset = false;
    }

    private static string StatusCss(SubchapterStatus status) => status switch
    {
        SubchapterStatus.Completed => "is-completed",
        SubchapterStatus.InProgress => "is-in-progress",
        _ => "is-not-started"
    };

    private static string StatusText(SubchapterStatus status) => status switch
    {
        SubchapterStatus.Completed => "Completed",
        SubchapterStatus.InProgress => "In progress",
        _ => "Not started"
    };

    private static string StatusGlyph(SubchapterStatus status) => status switch
    {
        SubchapterStatus.Completed => "✓",
        SubchapterStatus.InProgress => "◐",
        _ => "○"
    };
}

@page "/books/{BookSlug}/{ChapterSlug}/{SubchapterSlug}"
@inject IBookService BookService
@inject IReadingProgressService ProgressService
@inject IBookAnalytics BookAnalytics
@inject ILogger<BookReader> Logger

<PageTitle>@(_content?.Title ?? "Book") — Terlingua Magazine</PageTitle>

@if (_loading)
{
    <p><em>Loading...</em></p>
}
else if (_toc is null || _location is null || _content is null)
{
    <h1>Section Not Found</h1>
    <p>
        Sorry, we couldn't find that section.
        @if (_toc is not null)
        {
            <a href="books/@_toc.Slug">Back to @_toc.Title</a>
        }
        else
        {
            <a href="books">Back to Books</a>
        }
    </p>
}
else
{
    <nav class="reader-breadcrumbs" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
        <a href="books">Books</a>
        <span aria-hidden="true">›</span>
        <a href="books/@_toc.Slug">@_toc.Title</a>
        <span aria-hidden="true">›</span>
        <span>Chapter @_location.Chapter.Number: @_location.Chapter.Title</span>
    </nav>

    <article class="book-reader">
        <header class="reader-header">
            <h1><span class="reader-number">@_location.Label</span> @_content.Title</h1>
            <div class="blog-meta">

                @if (_content.Questions.Length > 0)
                {

                }
                @if (_readerCount.HasValue)
                {
                    <span> · @_readerCount @(_readerCount == 1 ? "reader" : "readers")</span>
                }
            </div>
            <ProgressMeter Percent="@_completion.Percent"
                           Label="@($"Book progress: {_completion.CompletedSubchapters} of {_completion.TotalSubchapters} sections complete")" />
        </header>

        <RichContent Html="@_content.Html" />

        <SubchapterQuiz @key="_location.ProgressKey"
                        BookSlug="@_toc.Slug"
                        Content="_content"
                        Progress="_subchapterProgress"
                        OnProgressChanged="ApplyProgress" />

        @if (!_isPersistent)
        {
            <p class="storage-note">This browser can't store progress, so it will reset when you leave the site.</p>
        }

        <nav class="reader-pager" aria-label="Section navigation">
            @if (_neighbors.Previous is { } previous)
            {
                <a class="reader-pager-link is-previous" href="@previous.Href(_toc.Slug)">
                    <span class="reader-pager-direction">&larr; Previous</span>
                    <span class="reader-pager-title">@previous.Label @previous.Subchapter.Title</span>
                </a>

            else
            {
                <a class="reader-pager-link is-previous" href="books/@_toc.Slug">
                    <span class="reader-pager-direction">&larr; Contents</span>
                    <span class="reader-pager-title">@_toc.Title</span>
                </a>
            }

            @if (_neighbors.Next is { } next)
            {
                <a class="reader-pager-link is-next" href="@next.Href(_toc.Slug)">
                    <span class="reader-pager-direction">Next &rarr;</span>
                    <span class="reader-pager-title">@next.Label @next.Subchapter.Title</span>
                </a>
            }
            else
            {
                <a class="reader-pager-link is-next" href="books/@_toc.Slug">
                    <span class="reader-pager-direction">Finished &rarr;</span>
                    <span class="reader-pager-title">Back to contents</span>
                </a>
            }
        </nav>
    </article>
}

@code {
    [Parameter] public string BookSlug { get; set; } = "";
    [Parameter] public string ChapterSlug { get; set; } = "";
    [Parameter] public string SubchapterSlug { get; set; } = "";

    private bool _loading = true;
    private BookToc? _toc;
    private SubchapterLocation? _location;
    private SubchapterContent? _content;
    private SubchapterNeighbors _neighbors = new(null, null);
    private SubchapterProgress? _subchapterProgress;
    private BookCompletion _completion = BookCompletion.Empty;
    private int? _readerCount;
    private bool _isPersistent = true;
    private string? _loadedKey;

    protected override async Task OnParametersSetAsync()
    {
        var key = $"{BookSlug}/{ChapterSlug}/{SubchapterSlug}";
        if (string.Equals(_loadedKey, key, StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            return;
        }

        _loadedKey = key;
        _loading = true;
        _readerCount = null;

        VisitOutcome? visit = null;
        try
        {
            visit = await LoadAsync();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Logger.LogError(ex, "Failed to load book section {Key}", key);
            _content = null;
        }
        finally
        {
            _loading = false;
        }

        if (visit is null || _toc is null || _location is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        StateHasChanged();
        await TrackVisitAsync(key, _toc.Slug, _location, visit);
    }

    private async Task<VisitOutcome?> LoadAsync()
    {
        _toc = await BookService.GetTocAsync(BookSlug);
        _location = _toc is null ? null : BookNavigator.Find(_toc, ChapterSlug, SubchapterSlug);
        _content = null;

        if (_toc is null || _location is null)
        {
            return null;
        }

        _content = await BookService.GetSubchapterAsync(_toc.Slug, _location.Chapter.Slug, _location.Subchapter.Slug);
        if (_content is null)
        {
            return null;
        }

        _neighbors = BookNavigator.GetNeighbors(_toc, _location);
        var visit = await ProgressService.RecordVisitAsync(_toc.Slug, _location.Chapter.Slug, _location.Subchapter.Slug);
        ApplyProgress(visit.Progress);
        _isPersistent = await ProgressService.IsPersistentAsync();
        return visit;
    }

    private async Task TrackVisitAsync(string key, string bookSlug, SubchapterLocation location, VisitOutcome visit)
    {
        if (visit.IsFirstVisitToBook)
        {
            await BookAnalytics.RecordBookStartedAsync(bookSlug);
        }

        await BookAnalytics.RecordSubchapterOpenedAsync(bookSlug, location.Chapter.Slug, location.Subchapter.Slug, visit.IsFirstVisitToSubchapter);
        var count = await BookAnalytics.GetSubchapterReaderCountAsync(bookSlug, location.Chapter.Slug, location.Subchapter.Slug);

        if (string.Equals(_loadedKey, key, StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            _readerCount = count;
        }
    }

    private void ApplyProgress(BookProgress progress)
    {
        if (_toc is null || _location is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        _subchapterProgress = progress.Subchapters.GetValueOrDefault(_location.ProgressKey);
        _completion = BookProgressCalculator.GetCompletion(_toc, progress);
    }
}

@page "/books"
@inject IBookService BookService
@inject IReadingProgressService ProgressService
@inject IBookAnalytics BookAnalytics
@inject IAnalyticsService Analytics
@inject ILogger<Books> Logger

<PageTitle>Books — Terlingua Magazine</PageTitle>

<h1>Books</h1>
<p class="books-intro">
    Read chapter by chapter and check your understanding with short quizzes.
    Your progress is saved in this browser.
</p>

@if (_books is null)
{
    <p><em>Loading books...</em></p>
}
else if (_books.Length == 0)
{
    <p>No books have been published yet.</p>
}
else
{
    <div class="book-list">
        @foreach (var book in _books)
        {
            var completion = _completions.GetValueOrDefault(book.Slug);
            var readers = _readerCounts.GetValueOrDefault(book.Slug);
            <article class="book-card">
                <h2><a href="books/@book.Slug">@book.Title</a></h2>
                <div class="blog-meta">
                    @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(book.AuthorName))
                    {
                        <span>@book.AuthorName · </span>
                    }
                    <span>@book.ChapterCount @(book.ChapterCount == 1 ? "chapter" : "chapters")</span>
                    <span> · @book.SubchapterCount sections</span>
                    @if (book.QuestionCount > 0)
                    {
                        <span> · @book.QuestionCount questions</span>
                    }
                    @if (book.ReadingTimeMinutes > 0)
                    {
                        <span> · @book.ReadingTimeMinutes min read</span>
                    }
                    @if (readers.HasValue)
                    {
                        <span> · @readers @(readers == 1 ? "reader" : "readers")</span>
                    }
                </div>
                @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(book.Summary))
                {
                    <p>@book.Summary</p>
                }
                @if (completion is not null)
                {
                    <ProgressMeter Percent="@completion.Percent"
                                   Label="@($"{completion.Percent}% complete · {completion.CompletedSubchapters} of {completion.TotalSubchapters} sections")" />
                }
                @if (book.Tags is { Length: > 0 })
                {
                    <div class="tag-list">
                        @foreach (var tag in book.Tags)
                        {
                            <span class="tag">@tag</span>
                        }
                    </div>
                }
            </article>
        }
    </div>
}

@code {
    private BookSummary[]? _books;
    private readonly Dictionary<string, BookCompletion> _completions = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    private readonly Dictionary<string, int?> _readerCounts = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        try
        {
            _books = await BookService.GetBooksAsync();
            await LoadCompletionsAsync(_books);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Logger.LogError(ex, "Failed to load books");
            _books ??= [];
        }

        StateHasChanged();
        await Analytics.TrackPageViewAsync("Books", $"{_books.Length} books");
        await LoadReaderCountsAsync(_books);
    }

    private async Task LoadCompletionsAsync(IEnumerable<BookSummary> books)
    {
        foreach (var book in books)
        {
            var progress = await ProgressService.GetAsync(book.Slug);
            if (!progress.IsStarted)
            {
                continue;
            }

            var toc = await BookService.GetTocAsync(book.Slug);
            if (toc is not null)
            {
                _completions[book.Slug] = BookProgressCalculator.GetCompletion(toc, progress);
            }
        }
    }

    private async Task LoadReaderCountsAsync(IEnumerable<BookSummary> books)
    {
        var lookups = books.Select(async b => (b.Slug, Count: await BookAnalytics.GetReaderCountAsync(b.Slug)));
        foreach (var (slug, count) in await Task.WhenAll(lookups))
        {
            if (count.HasValue)
            {
                _readerCounts[slug] = count;
            }
        }
    }
}

        <p>Light, Dark, Sepia, Solarized, and High Contrast — your choice, persisted locally.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="feature-card">
        <h3>Full Test Suite</h3>
        <p>bUnit component tests and xUnit integration tests run on every commit.</p>
    </div>
</section>

@code {
    private BlogPostMetadata[]? _featuredPosts;

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        await Analytics.TrackPageViewAsync("Home");

        try
        {
            var allPosts = await BlogService.GetPostsAsync();
            _featuredPosts = allPosts.Where(p => p.Featured).Take(3).ToArray();
        }
        catch
        {
            _featuredPosts = [];
        }
    }
}

@inject IJSRuntime Js

<PageTitle>@(IsNew ? "New Product" : (_editing ? "Edit Product" : _product?.Name ?? "Product")) — Showcase</PageTitle>

            </dl>

            <div class="form-actions" style="margin-top:1.5rem;">
                <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" @onclick="StartEdit">Edit</button>
                <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" @onclick="ConfirmDeleteProduct">Delete</button>
            </div>
        }
        else
        {
            @* ---- Edit / Create mode ---- *@
            <h2>@(IsNew ? "New Product" : $"Editing: {_product!.Name}")</h2>

                <label for="f-name">Name</label>

                @if (_showVal && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_editName))
                {
                    <div class="form-validation">Name is required.</div>
                }
            </div>
            <div class="sd-form-row">

                    <input id="f-sku" @bind="_editSku" @bind:event="oninput" />
                </div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="f-brand">Brand</label>
                    <input id="f-brand" @bind="_editBrand" @bind:event="oninput" />
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="sd-form-row">
                <div class="form-group">

                    <select id="f-cat" @bind="_editCategory">

            <div class="sd-form-row">

                    <input id="f-price" type="number" step="0.01" min="0" @bind="_editPrice" />

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="f-stock">Stock</label>

                </div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="f-color">Color</label>
                    <input id="f-color" @bind="_editColor" @bind:event="oninput" />
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="sd-form-row">
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="f-rating">Rating (0–5)</label>
                    <input id="f-rating" type="number" step="0.1" min="0" max="5" @bind="_editRating" />
                </div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="f-reviews">Review Count</label>
                    <input id="f-reviews" type="number" min="0" @bind="_editReviewCount" />
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="sd-form-row">
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="f-weight">Weight (kg)</label>
                    <input id="f-weight" type="number" step="0.01" min="0" @bind="_editWeight" />
                </div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="f-warranty">Warranty (months)</label>
                    <input id="f-warranty" type="number" min="0" @bind="_editWarranty" />
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="form-group">
                <label for="f-desc">Description</label>
                <textarea id="f-desc" @bind="_editDescription" @bind:event="oninput"></textarea>
            </div>

            <div class="form-actions">
                <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" @onclick="Save">Save</button>
                <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="CancelEdit">Cancel</button>
            </div>
        }
    </div>
}

@* ---- Delete confirm ---- *@
@if (_showDeleteConfirm)
{
    <div class="sd-overlay" @onclick="() => _showDeleteConfirm = false">
        <div class="sd-dialog" @onclick:stopPropagation="true">
            <h4>Delete Product</h4>
            <p>Are you sure you want to delete <strong>@_product?.Name</strong>? This cannot be undone.</p>
            <div class="form-actions">
                <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="() => _showDeleteConfirm = false">Cancel</button>
                <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" @onclick="ExecuteDelete">Delete</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
}

@code {
    // MUST match Showcase.razor's StorageKey so both pages share the same data
    private const string StorageKey = "observer-catalog-products";

    [Parameter] public Guid? ProductId { get; set; }

    private bool IsNew => ProductId is null;
    private bool _loading = true;
    private bool _editing;
    private bool _showVal;
    private bool _showDeleteConfirm;

    private List<CatalogProduct> _allProducts = [];
    private CatalogProduct? _product;

    // Edit fields
    private string _editName = "", _editSku = "", _editBrand = "", _editCategory = "Electronics",
        _editStatus = "Active", _editColor = "", _editDescription = "";
    private decimal _editPrice, _editComparePrice;
    private int _editStock, _editReviewCount, _editWarranty;
    private double _editRating, _editWeight;

    protected override async Task OnParametersSetAsync()
    {
        _loading = true;
        await LoadAll();

        if (IsNew)
        {
            _product = null;
            _editing = true;
            ResetFormFields();
        }
        else
        {
            _product = _allProducts.FirstOrDefault(p => p.Id == ProductId);
            _editing = false;
        }

        _loading = false;
    }

    private async Task LoadAll()
    {
        try
        {
            var saved = await Js.InvokeAsync<string?>("localStorage.getItem", StorageKey);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(saved))
            {
                var deserialized = System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Deserialize<List<CatalogProduct>>(saved,
                    new System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions { PropertyNameCaseInsensitive = true });
                if (deserialized is { Count: > 0 })
                {
                    _allProducts = deserialized;
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
        catch { /* best effort */ }

        _allProducts = ProductDataGenerator.Generate();
        await SaveAll();
    }

    private async Task SaveAll()
    {
        try
        {
            var json = System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Serialize(_allProducts,
                new System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions { PropertyNamingPolicy = System.Text.Json.JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase });
            await Js.InvokeVoidAsync("localStorage.setItem", StorageKey, json);
        }
        catch { /* best effort */ }
    }

    private void StartEdit()
    {
        if (_product is null) return;
        _editName = _product.Name;
        _editSku = _product.Sku;
        _editBrand = _product.Brand;
        _editCategory = _product.Category;
        _editStatus = _product.Status;
        _editPrice = _product.Price;
        _editComparePrice = _product.CompareAtPrice ?? 0;
        _editStock = _product.Stock;
        _editRating = _product.Rating;
        _editReviewCount = _product.ReviewCount;
        _editWeight = _product.WeightKg;
        _editColor = _product.Color;
        _editWarranty = _product.WarrantyMonths;
        _editDescription = _product.Description;
        _showVal = false;
        _editing = true;
    }

    private void ResetFormFields()
    {
        _editName = ""; _editSku = ""; _editBrand = ""; _editCategory = "Electronics";
        _editStatus = "Active"; _editColor = ""; _editDescription = "";
        _editPrice = 0; _editComparePrice = 0; _editStock = 0; _editReviewCount = 0;
        _editRating = 0; _editWeight = 0; _editWarranty = 0;
        _showVal = false;
    }

    private async Task Save()
    {
        _showVal = true;
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_editName)) return;

        if (IsNew)
        {
            var newP = new CatalogProduct
            {
                Id = Guid.NewGuid(),
                Sku = _editSku.Trim(),
                Name = _editName.Trim(),
                Brand = _editBrand.Trim(),
                Category = _editCategory,
                Price = _editPrice,
                CompareAtPrice = _editComparePrice > 0 ? _editComparePrice : null,
                Stock = _editStock,
                Rating = Math.Clamp(_editRating, 0, 5),
                ReviewCount = _editReviewCount,
                WeightKg = _editWeight,
                Color = _editColor.Trim(),
                WarrantyMonths = _editWarranty,
                DateAdded = DateTime.UtcNow,
                Status = _editStatus,
                Description = _editDescription.Trim()
            };
            _allProducts.Add(newP);
            await SaveAll();
            Nav.NavigateTo($"showcase/{newP.Id}");
        }
        else if (_product is not null)
        {
            var idx = _allProducts.FindIndex(p => p.Id == _product.Id);
            if (idx >= 0)
            {
                _allProducts[idx] = new CatalogProduct
                {
                    Id = _product.Id,
                    Sku = _editSku.Trim(),
                    Name = _editName.Trim(),
                    Brand = _editBrand.Trim(),
                    Category = _editCategory,
                    Price = _editPrice,
                    CompareAtPrice = _editComparePrice > 0 ? _editComparePrice : null,
                    Stock = _editStock,
                    Rating = Math.Clamp(_editRating, 0, 5),
                    ReviewCount = _editReviewCount,
                    WeightKg = _editWeight,
                    Color = _editColor.Trim(),
                    WarrantyMonths = _editWarranty,
                    DateAdded = _product.DateAdded,
                    Status = _editStatus,
                    Description = _editDescription.Trim()
                };
                _product = _allProducts[idx];
            }
            await SaveAll();
            _editing = false;
            _showVal = false;
        }
    }

    private void CancelEdit()
    {
        if (IsNew)
            Nav.NavigateTo("showcase");
        else
            _editing = false;
    }

    private void ConfirmDeleteProduct() => _showDeleteConfirm = true;

    private async Task ExecuteDelete()
    {
        if (_product is not null)
        {
            _allProducts.RemoveAll(p => p.Id == _product.Id);
            await SaveAll();
        }
        Nav.NavigateTo("showcase");
    }

    private void BackToList() => Nav.NavigateTo("showcase");
}

@page "/showcase"
@inject ILogger<Showcase> Logger
@inject IAnalyticsService Analytics
@inject IJSRuntime Js
@inject NavigationManager Nav
@implements IDisposable

<PageTitle>Showcase — Terlingua Magazine</PageTitle>

<h1>Product Catalog Showcase</h1>
<p>
    A paginated, sortable, filterable product catalog with column visibility controls and
    full CRUD. Demonstrates patterns real e-commerce applications need. Data is persisted in
    your browser's local storage.
</p>

@* ---- Toolbar ---- *@
<div class="sc-toolbar">
    <div class="sc-toolbar-left">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" @onclick="NavigateToNew">+ New Product</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="ResetData">Reset Data</button>
        <span class="sc-count">@_filteredProducts.Count of @_allProducts.Count items</span>
    </div>
    <div class="sc-toolbar-right">
        <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="ToggleFilters">
            @(_showFilters ? "Hide Filters" : "Show Filters")
        </button>
        <div class="sc-col-toggle">
            <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="ToggleColumnPicker">Columns ▾</button>
            @if (_showColumnPicker)
            {
                <div class="sc-col-backdrop" @onclick="() => _showColumnPicker = false"></div>
                <div class="sc-col-dropdown" @onclick:stopPropagation="true">
                    @foreach (var col in _columns)
                    {
                        <label class="sc-col-option">
                            <input type="checkbox" checked="@col.Visible"
                                   @onchange="e => ToggleColumn(col, (bool)(e.Value ?? false))" />
                            @col.Header
                        </label>
                    }
                    <hr />
                    <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm sc-col-restore" @onclick="RestoreColumnDefaults">
                        Restore Defaults
                    </button>
                </div>
            }
        </div>
        <div class="sc-page-size">
            <label>Per page:</label>
            <select value="@_pageSize" @onchange="OnPageSizeChanged">
                <option value="10">10</option>
                <option value="20">20</option>
                <option value="50">50</option>
                <option value="100">100</option>
            </select>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@* ---- Filter panel (collapsible, works on desktop + mobile) ---- *@
@if (_showFilters)
{
    <div class="sc-filter-panel">
        <div class="sc-filter-panel-header">
            <strong>Filters</strong>
            <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="ClearFilters" title="Clear all filters">
                Clear All Filters
            </button>
        </div>
        <div class="sc-filter-grid">
            @foreach (var col in _columns.Where(c => c.Visible))
            {
                @if (col.FilterKind == FilterKind.Text)
                {
                    <div class="sc-filter-row">
                        <label class="sc-filter-label">@col.Header</label>
                        <input type="text" class="sc-filter-input"
                               placeholder="Filter @col.Header..."
                               value="@col.FilterText"
                               @oninput="e => OnFilterTextChanged(col, e.Value?.ToString() ?? string.Empty)" />
                    </div>
                }
                else if (col.FilterKind == FilterKind.Range)
                {
                    <div class="sc-filter-row">
                        <label class="sc-filter-label">@col.Header</label>
                        <div class="sc-range-inputs">
                            <input type="number" class="sc-filter-input"
                                   placeholder="Min"
                                   value="@col.FilterMin"
                                   @oninput="e => OnFilterMinChanged(col, e.Value?.ToString() ?? string.Empty)" />
                            <span class="sc-range-sep">–</span>
                            <input type="number" class="sc-filter-input"
                                   placeholder="Max"
                                   value="@col.FilterMax"
                                   @oninput="e => OnFilterMaxChanged(col, e.Value?.ToString() ?? string.Empty)" />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                }
                else if (col.FilterKind == FilterKind.Select)
                {
                    <div class="sc-filter-row">
                        <label class="sc-filter-label">@col.Header</label>
                        <select class="sc-filter-input"
                                value="@col.FilterText"
                                @onchange="e => OnFilterTextChanged(col, e.Value?.ToString() ?? string.Empty)">
                            <option value="">All</option>
                            @foreach (var opt in col.SelectOptions)
                            {
                                <option value="@opt">@opt</option>
                            }
                        </select>
                    </div>
                }
            }
        </div>
    </div>
}

@* ---- Table ---- *@
<div class="sc-table-wrap">
    <table class="sc-table">
        <thead>
            <tr>
                @foreach (var col in _columns.Where(c => c.Visible))
                {
                    <th class="@(col.Numeric ? "sc-num" : string.Empty) sc-sortable"
                        style="@ColStyle(col)"
                        @onclick="() => SortBy(col)">
                        @col.Header
                        @if (_sortColumn == col.Key)
                        {
                            <span class="sc-sort-arrow">@(_sortAsc ? "▲" : "▼")</span>
                        }
                    </th>
                }
                <th class="sc-actions-col">Actions</th>
            </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
            @if (_pagedProducts.Count == 0)
            {
                <tr>
                    <td colspan="@(_columns.Count(c => c.Visible) + 1)" class="sc-empty">
                        No products match your filters.
                    </td>
                </tr>
            }
            @foreach (var p in _pagedProducts)
            {
                <tr @onclick="() => NavigateToDetail(p.Id)" class="sc-clickable-row">
                    @foreach (var col in _columns.Where(c => c.Visible))
                    {
                        <td class="@(col.Numeric ? "sc-num" : string.Empty)"
                            data-label="@col.Header">
                            @col.Render(p)
                        </td>
                    }
                    <td class="sc-actions" data-label="Actions" @onclick:stopPropagation="true">
                        <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="() => NavigateToDetail(p.Id)">Edit</button>
                        <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" @onclick="() => ConfirmDelete(p)">Del</button>
                    </td>
                </tr>
            }
        </tbody>
    </table>
</div>

@* ---- Pagination ---- *@
<div class="sc-pagination">
    <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="FirstPage" disabled="@(_currentPage <= 1)">« First</button>
    <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="PrevPage" disabled="@(_currentPage <= 1)">‹ Prev</button>
    <span class="sc-page-info">Page @_currentPage of @_totalPages</span>
    <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="NextPage" disabled="@(_currentPage >= _totalPages)">Next ›</button>
    <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="LastPage" disabled="@(_currentPage >= _totalPages)">Last »</button>
</div>

@* ---- Delete confirmation overlay ---- *@
@if (_deleteTarget is not null)
{
    <div class="sc-overlay" @onclick="CancelDelete">
        <div class="sc-dialog" @onclick:stopPropagation="true">
            <h4>Delete product?</h4>
            <p>Are you sure you want to delete <strong>@_deleteTarget.Name</strong> (@_deleteTarget.Sku)?</p>
            <div style="display:flex;gap:0.5rem;justify-content:flex-end;">
                <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" @onclick="CancelDelete">Cancel</button>
                <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" @onclick="ExecuteDelete">Delete</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
}

@code {
    private const string StorageKey = "observer-catalog-products";
    private const string PrefsKey = "observer-showcase-prefs";

    private List<CatalogProduct> _allProducts = [];
    private List<CatalogProduct> _filteredProducts = [];
    private List<CatalogProduct> _pagedProducts = [];

    private int _pageSize = 20;
    private int _currentPage = 1;
    private int _totalPages = 1;
    private string _sortColumn = "Name";
    private bool _sortAsc = true;
    private bool _showColumnPicker;
    private bool _showFilters = true;
    private CatalogProduct? _deleteTarget;

    // --- Column definitions ---
    private List<ColumnDef> _columns = [];

    // Track default visibility for "Restore Defaults"
    private Dictionary<string, bool> _defaultVisibility = new();

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        InitColumns();
        await LoadProducts();
        await LoadPrefs();
        ApplyFilterAndSort();
        await Analytics.TrackPageViewAsync("Showcase");
    }

    private void InitColumns()
    {
        _columns =
        [
            new("Sku", "SKU", p => p.Sku, false, FilterKind.Text) { MinWidth = "90px" },
            new("Name", "Name", p => p.Name, false, FilterKind.Text) { MinWidth = "160px" },
            new("Brand", "Brand", p => p.Brand, false, FilterKind.Text) { MinWidth = "100px" },
            new("Category", "Category", p => p.Category, false, FilterKind.Select)
            {
                MinWidth = "120px",
                SelectOptions = ["Electronics", "Furniture", "Clothing", "Home & Kitchen", "Sports & Outdoors", "Office Supplies", "Accessories"]
            },
            new("Price", "Price", p => p.Price.ToString("C"), true, FilterKind.Range) { MinWidth = "100px" },
            new("Stock", "Stock", p => p.Stock.ToString(), true, FilterKind.Range) { MinWidth = "80px" },
            new("Rating", "Rating", p => p.Rating.ToString("F1"), true, FilterKind.Range) { MinWidth = "70px" },
            new("ReviewCount", "Reviews", p => p.ReviewCount.ToString("N0"), true, FilterKind.Range) { MinWidth = "80px", Visible = false },
            new("WeightKg", "Weight (kg)", p => p.WeightKg.ToString("F2"), true, FilterKind.Range) { MinWidth = "90px", Visible = false },
            new("Color", "Color", p => p.Color, false, FilterKind.Text) { MinWidth = "90px", Visible = false },
            new("WarrantyMonths", "Warranty", p => p.WarrantyMonths > 0 ? $"{p.WarrantyMonths} mo" : "None", true, FilterKind.Range)
            {
                MinWidth = "80px",
                Visible = false
            },
            new("DateAdded", "Date Added", p => p.DateAdded.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd"), false, FilterKind.Text)
            {
                MinWidth = "100px",
                Visible = false
            },
            new("Status", "Status", p => p.Status, false, FilterKind.Select)
            {
                MinWidth = "90px",
                SelectOptions = ["Active", "Draft", "Archived", "Out of Stock"]
            },
        ];

        // Save defaults
        _defaultVisibility = _columns.ToDictionary(c => c.Key, c => c.Visible);
    }

    // --- Product persistence ---

    private async Task LoadProducts()
    {
        try
        {
            var json = await Js.InvokeAsync<string?>("localStorage.getItem", StorageKey);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(json))
            {
                var loaded = System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Deserialize<List<CatalogProduct>>(json,
                    new System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions { PropertyNamingPolicy = System.Text.Json.JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase });
                if (loaded is { Count: > 0 })
                {
                    _allProducts = loaded;
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Logger.LogWarning(ex, "Could not load catalog from local storage");
        }

        _allProducts = ProductDataGenerator.Generate();
        await SaveProducts();
    }

    private async Task SaveProducts()
    {
        try
        {
            var json = System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Serialize(_allProducts,
                new System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions { PropertyNamingPolicy = System.Text.Json.JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase });
            await Js.InvokeVoidAsync("localStorage.setItem", StorageKey, json);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Logger.LogWarning(ex, "Could not save catalog to local storage");
        }
    }

    // --- Preferences persistence ---

    private async Task LoadPrefs()
    {
        try
        {
            var json = await Js.InvokeAsync<string?>("localStorage.getItem", PrefsKey);
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(json)) return;

            var prefs = System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Deserialize<ShowcasePrefs>(json,
                new System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions { PropertyNamingPolicy = System.Text.Json.JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase });
            if (prefs is null) return;

            if (prefs.PageSize is > 0) _pageSize = prefs.PageSize.Value;
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(prefs.SortColumn)) _sortColumn = prefs.SortColumn;
            if (prefs.SortAsc is not null) _sortAsc = prefs.SortAsc.Value;
            if (prefs.ShowFilters is not null) _showFilters = prefs.ShowFilters.Value;

            if (prefs.ColumnVisibility is not null)
            {
                foreach (var col in _columns)
                {
                    if (prefs.ColumnVisibility.TryGetValue(col.Key, out var vis))
                        col.Visible = vis;
                }
            }

            if (prefs.Filters is not null)
            {
                foreach (var col in _columns)
                {
                    if (prefs.Filters.TryGetValue(col.Key, out var f))
                    {
                        col.FilterText = f.Text ?? string.Empty;
                        col.FilterMin = f.Min ?? string.Empty;
                        col.FilterMax = f.Max ?? string.Empty;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Logger.LogDebug(ex, "Could not load showcase preferences");
        }
    }

    private async Task SavePrefs()
    {
        try
        {
            var prefs = new ShowcasePrefs
            {
                PageSize = _pageSize,
                SortColumn = _sortColumn,
                SortAsc = _sortAsc,
                ShowFilters = _showFilters,
                ColumnVisibility = _columns.ToDictionary(c => c.Key, c => c.Visible),
                Filters = _columns
                    .Where(c => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(c.FilterText) || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(c.FilterMin) || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(c.FilterMax))
                    .ToDictionary(c => c.Key, c => new FilterState
                    {
                        Text = string.IsNullOrEmpty(c.FilterText) ? null : c.FilterText,
                        Min = string.IsNullOrEmpty(c.FilterMin) ? null : c.FilterMin,
                        Max = string.IsNullOrEmpty(c.FilterMax) ? null : c.FilterMax,
                    })
            };

            var json = System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Serialize(prefs,
                new System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions { PropertyNamingPolicy = System.Text.Json.JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase });
            await Js.InvokeVoidAsync("localStorage.setItem", PrefsKey, json);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Logger.LogDebug(ex, "Could not save showcase preferences");
        }
    }

    // --- Filter / sort / paginate ---

    private void ApplyFilterAndSort()
    {
        IEnumerable<CatalogProduct> result = _allProducts;

        foreach (var col in _columns)
        {
            if (col.FilterKind == FilterKind.Text && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(col.FilterText))
            {
                var term = col.FilterText;
                result = col.Key switch
                {
                    "Sku" => result.Where(p => p.Sku.Contains(term, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)),
                    "Name" => result.Where(p => p.Name.Contains(term, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)),
                    "Brand" => result.Where(p => p.Brand.Contains(term, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)),
                    "Color" => result.Where(p => p.Color.Contains(term, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)),
                    "DateAdded" => result.Where(p => p.DateAdded.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd").Contains(term, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)),
                    _ => result
                };
            }
            else if (col.FilterKind == FilterKind.Select && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(col.FilterText))
            {
                var term = col.FilterText;
                result = col.Key switch
                {
                    "Category" => result.Where(p => p.Category.Equals(term, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)),
                    "Status" => result.Where(p => p.Status.Equals(term, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)),
                    _ => result
                };
            }
            else if (col.FilterKind == FilterKind.Range)
            {
                if (double.TryParse(col.FilterMin, out var min))
                {
                    result = col.Key switch
                    {
                        "Price" => result.Where(p => (double)p.Price >= min),
                        "Stock" => result.Where(p => p.Stock >= min),
                        "Rating" => result.Where(p => p.Rating >= min),
                        "ReviewCount" => result.Where(p => p.ReviewCount >= min),
                        "WeightKg" => result.Where(p => p.WeightKg >= min),
                        "WarrantyMonths" => result.Where(p => p.WarrantyMonths >= min),
                        _ => result
                    };
                }

                if (double.TryParse(col.FilterMax, out var max))
                {
                    result = col.Key switch
                    {
                        "Price" => result.Where(p => (double)p.Price <= max),
                        "Stock" => result.Where(p => p.Stock <= max),
                        "Rating" => result.Where(p => p.Rating <= max),
                        "ReviewCount" => result.Where(p => p.ReviewCount <= max),
                        "WeightKg" => result.Where(p => p.WeightKg <= max),
                        "WarrantyMonths" => result.Where(p => p.WarrantyMonths <= max),
                        _ => result
                    };
                }
            }
        }

        // Sort
        result = _sortColumn switch
        {
            "Sku" => _sortAsc ? result.OrderBy(p => p.Sku) : result.OrderByDescending(p => p.Sku),
            "Name" => _sortAsc ? result.OrderBy(p => p.Name) : result.OrderByDescending(p => p.Name),
            "Brand" => _sortAsc ? result.OrderBy(p => p.Brand) : result.OrderByDescending(p => p.Brand),
            "Category" => _sortAsc ? result.OrderBy(p => p.Category) : result.OrderByDescending(p => p.Category),
            "Price" => _sortAsc ? result.OrderBy(p => p.Price) : result.OrderByDescending(p => p.Price),
            "Stock" => _sortAsc ? result.OrderBy(p => p.Stock) : result.OrderByDescending(p => p.Stock),
            "Rating" => _sortAsc ? result.OrderBy(p => p.Rating) : result.OrderByDescending(p => p.Rating),
            "ReviewCount" => _sortAsc ? result.OrderBy(p => p.ReviewCount) : result.OrderByDescending(p => p.ReviewCount),
            "WeightKg" => _sortAsc ? result.OrderBy(p => p.WeightKg) : result.OrderByDescending(p => p.WeightKg),
            "Color" => _sortAsc ? result.OrderBy(p => p.Color) : result.OrderByDescending(p => p.Color),
            "WarrantyMonths" => _sortAsc ? result.OrderBy(p => p.WarrantyMonths) : result.OrderByDescending(p => p.WarrantyMonths),
            "DateAdded" => _sortAsc ? result.OrderBy(p => p.DateAdded) : result.OrderByDescending(p => p.DateAdded),
            "Status" => _sortAsc ? result.OrderBy(p => p.Status) : result.OrderByDescending(p => p.Status),
            _ => result
        };

        _filteredProducts = result.ToList();
        _totalPages = Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Ceiling(_filteredProducts.Count / (double)_pageSize));
        if (_currentPage > _totalPages) _currentPage = _totalPages;
        if (_currentPage < 1) _currentPage = 1;

        _pagedProducts = _filteredProducts
            .Skip((_currentPage - 1) * _pageSize)
            .Take(_pageSize)
            .ToList();
    }

    // --- Event handlers ---

    private async Task SortBy(ColumnDef col)
    {
        if (_sortColumn == col.Key)
            _sortAsc = !_sortAsc;
        else
        {
            _sortColumn = col.Key;
            _sortAsc = true;
        }
        _currentPage = 1;
        ApplyFilterAndSort();
        await SavePrefs();
    }

    private async Task OnFilterTextChanged(ColumnDef col, string value)
    {
        col.FilterText = value;
        _currentPage = 1;
        ApplyFilterAndSort();
        await SavePrefs();
    }

    private async Task OnFilterMinChanged(ColumnDef col, string value)
    {
        col.FilterMin = value;
        _currentPage = 1;
        ApplyFilterAndSort();
        await SavePrefs();
    }

    private async Task OnFilterMaxChanged(ColumnDef col, string value)
    {
        col.FilterMax = value;
        _currentPage = 1;
        ApplyFilterAndSort();
        await SavePrefs();
    }

    private async Task ClearFilters()
    {
        foreach (var col in _columns)
        {
            col.FilterText = string.Empty;
            col.FilterMin = string.Empty;
            col.FilterMax = string.Empty;
        }
        _currentPage = 1;
        ApplyFilterAndSort();
        await SavePrefs();
    }

    private async Task OnPageSizeChanged(ChangeEventArgs e)
    {
        if (int.TryParse(e.Value?.ToString(), out var size) && size > 0)
        {
            _pageSize = size;
            _currentPage = 1;
            ApplyFilterAndSort();
            await SavePrefs();
        }
    }

    private void FirstPage() { _currentPage = 1; ApplyFilterAndSort(); }
    private void PrevPage() { if (_currentPage > 1) { _currentPage--; ApplyFilterAndSort(); } }
    private void NextPage() { if (_currentPage < _totalPages) { _currentPage++; ApplyFilterAndSort(); } }
    private void LastPage() { _currentPage = _totalPages; ApplyFilterAndSort(); }

    private async Task ToggleFilters()
    {
        _showFilters = !_showFilters;
        await SavePrefs();
    }

    private void ToggleColumnPicker() => _showColumnPicker = !_showColumnPicker;

    private async Task ToggleColumn(ColumnDef col, bool visible)
    {
        col.Visible = visible;
        await SavePrefs();
    }

    private async Task RestoreColumnDefaults()
    {
        foreach (var col in _columns)
        {
            if (_defaultVisibility.TryGetValue(col.Key, out var vis))
                col.Visible = vis;
            col.FilterText = string.Empty;
            col.FilterMin = string.Empty;
            col.FilterMax = string.Empty;
        }
        _sortColumn = "Name";
        _sortAsc = true;
        _pageSize = 20;
        _currentPage = 1;
        _showColumnPicker = false;
        ApplyFilterAndSort();
        await SavePrefs();
    }

    private void NavigateToDetail(Guid id) => Nav.NavigateTo($"showcase/{id}");
    private void NavigateToNew() => Nav.NavigateTo("showcase/new");

    private void ConfirmDelete(CatalogProduct p) => _deleteTarget = p;
    private void CancelDelete() => _deleteTarget = null;

    private async Task ExecuteDelete()
    {
        if (_deleteTarget is not null)
        {
            _allProducts.RemoveAll(p => p.Id == _deleteTarget.Id);
            _deleteTarget = null;
            ApplyFilterAndSort();
            await SaveProducts();
        }
    }

    private async Task ResetData()
    {
        try { await Js.InvokeVoidAsync("localStorage.removeItem", StorageKey); }
        catch { /* best effort */ }

        _allProducts = ProductDataGenerator.Generate();
        _currentPage = 1;
        await ClearFilters();
        await SaveProducts();
    }

    private static string ColStyle(ColumnDef col) =>
        string.IsNullOrEmpty(col.MinWidth) ? string.Empty : $"min-width:{col.MinWidth}";

    public void Dispose()
    {
        // Nothing to dispose for now, but good practice to implement IDisposable
    }

    // --- Column definition model ---

    public enum FilterKind { Text, Range, Select }

    public sealed class ColumnDef(string key, string header, Func<CatalogProduct, string> render, bool numeric, FilterKind filterKind)
    {
        public string Key { get; } = key;
        public string Header { get; } = header;
        public bool Numeric { get; } = numeric;
        public FilterKind FilterKind { get; } = filterKind;
        public bool Visible { get; set; } = true;
        public string FilterText { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string FilterMin { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string FilterMax { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string MinWidth { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string[] SelectOptions { get; set; } = [];
        public string Render(CatalogProduct p) => render(p);
    }

    // --- Preferences DTO ---

    public sealed class ShowcasePrefs
    {
        public int? PageSize { get; set; }
        public string? SortColumn { get; set; }
        public bool? SortAsc { get; set; }
        public bool? ShowFilters { get; set; }
        public Dictionary<string, bool>? ColumnVisibility { get; set; }
        public Dictionary<string, FilterState>? Filters { get; set; }
    }

    public sealed class FilterState
    {
        public string? Text { get; set; }
        public string? Min { get; set; }
        public string? Max { get; set; }
    }
}

FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Program.cs

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Components.Web;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Components.WebAssembly.Hosting;
using ObserverMagazine.Web;
using ObserverMagazine.Web.Services;

var builder = WebAssemblyHostBuilder.CreateDefault(args);
builder.RootComponents.Add<App>("#app");
builder.RootComponents.Add<HeadOutlet>("head::after");

builder.Services.AddScoped(_ =>
    new HttpClient { BaseAddress = new Uri(builder.HostEnvironment.BaseAddress) });

builder.Services.AddSingleton(TimeProvider.System);
builder.Services.AddScoped<IBlogService, BlogService>();
builder.Services.AddSingleton<TelemetryService>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<IAnalyticsService, AnalyticsService>();

builder.Services.AddScoped<IBookService, BookService>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<IBookAnalytics, BookAnalytics>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<IBrowserStorage, LocalBrowserStorage>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<LocalStorageProgressStore>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<IProgressStore>(sp => new FallbackProgressStore(
    sp.GetRequiredService<LocalStorageProgressStore>(),
    new InMemoryProgressStore(),
    sp.GetRequiredService<ILogger<FallbackProgressStore>>()));
builder.Services.AddScoped<IReadingProgressService, ReadingProgressService>();

builder.Logging.SetMinimumLevel(LogLevel.Information);

var host = builder.Build();

var logger = host.Services.GetRequiredService<ILoggerFactory>().CreateLogger("App");
logger.LogInformation("Terlingua Magazine started at {Time}", DateTime.UtcNow);

var telemetry = host.Services.GetRequiredService<TelemetryService>();
telemetry.TrackEvent("AppStarted");

await host.RunAsync();

FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Services/AnalyticsService.cs
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
using System.Net.Http.Json;

namespace ObserverMagazine.Web.Services;

public sealed class AnalyticsService(HttpClient http, TimeProvider time, ILogger<AnalyticsService> logger) : IAnalyticsService
{
    private const string BackendBaseUrl = "https://my-api.2w7sp317.workers.dev";
    private static readonly TimeSpan HealthCacheDuration = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5);

    private bool _backendAvailable;
    private DateTimeOffset? _lastHealthCheck;

    public bool IsBackendAvailable => _backendAvailable;

    public async Task CheckHealthAsync()
    {
        if (_lastHealthCheck is { } last && time.GetUtcNow() - last < HealthCacheDuration)
        {
            return;
        }

        try
        {
            var response = await http.GetAsync($"{BackendBaseUrl}/api/health");
            _backendAvailable = response.IsSuccessStatusCode;
            logger.LogInformation("Backend health check: {Status}", _backendAvailable ? "available" : "unavailable");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _backendAvailable = false;
            logger.LogDebug(ex, "Backend health check failed — running without backend");
        }
        finally
        {
            _lastHealthCheck = time.GetUtcNow();
        }
    }

    public async Task TrackPageViewAsync(string pageName, string? detail = null)
    {
        await EnsureHealthChecked();
        if (!_backendAvailable) return;

        var content = detail is not null
            ? $"[PageView] {pageName} — {detail}"
            : $"[PageView] {pageName}";

        await SendEventAsync($"PageView: {pageName}", content);
    }

    public async Task TrackInteractionAsync(string action, string? detail = null)
    {
        await EnsureHealthChecked();
        if (!_backendAvailable) return;

        var content = detail is not null
            ? $"[Interaction] {action} — {detail}"
            : $"[Interaction] {action}";

        await SendEventAsync($"Interaction: {action}", content);
    }

    public async Task IncrementViewAsync(string slug)
    {
        await EnsureHealthChecked();
        if (!_backendAvailable) return;

        try
        {
            await http.PostAsync($"{BackendBaseUrl}/api/views/{slug}", null);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            logger.LogDebug(ex, "Failed to increment view for {Slug}", slug);
        }
    }

    public async Task<int?> GetViewCountAsync(string slug)
    {
        await EnsureHealthChecked();
        if (!_backendAvailable) return null;

        try
        {
            var result = await http.GetFromJsonAsync<ViewCountResponse>($"{BackendBaseUrl}/api/views/{slug}");
            return result?.Count;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            logger.LogDebug(ex, "Failed to get view count for {Slug}", slug);
            return null;
        }
    }

    public async Task AddReactionAsync(string slug, string reactionType)
    {
        await EnsureHealthChecked();
        if (!_backendAvailable) return;

        try
        {
            var payload = new { type = reactionType };
            await http.PostAsJsonAsync($"{BackendBaseUrl}/api/reactions/{slug}", payload);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            logger.LogDebug(ex, "Failed to add reaction for {Slug}", slug);
        }
    }

    public async Task<Dictionary<string, int>?> GetReactionsAsync(string slug)
    {
        await EnsureHealthChecked();
        if (!_backendAvailable) return null;

        try
        {
            return await http.GetFromJsonAsync<Dictionary<string, int>>($"{BackendBaseUrl}/api/reactions/{slug}");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            logger.LogDebug(ex, "Failed to get reactions for {Slug}", slug);
            return null;
        }
    }

    private async Task EnsureHealthChecked()
    {
        if (_lastHealthCheck is null)
        {
            await CheckHealthAsync();
        }
    }

    private async Task SendEventAsync(string title, string content)
    {
        try
        {
            var payload = new { title, content };
            var response = await http.PostAsJsonAsync($"{BackendBaseUrl}/api/notes", payload);

            if (!response.IsSuccessStatusCode)
            {
                logger.LogDebug("Analytics POST returned {StatusCode}", response.StatusCode);
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            logger.LogDebug(ex, "Failed to send analytics event: {Title}", title);
        }
    }

    private sealed record ViewCountResponse(int Count);
}

FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Services/BlogService.cs
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using ObserverMagazine.Web.Models;

namespace ObserverMagazine.Web.Services;

public sealed class BlogService(HttpClient http, ILogger<BlogService> logger) : IBlogService
{
    private BlogPostMetadata[]? _cachedIndex;
    private AuthorProfile[]? _cachedAuthors;

    public async Task<BlogPostMetadata[]> GetPostsAsync()
    {
        if (_cachedIndex is not null) return _cachedIndex;

        logger.LogInformation("Fetching blog posts index");
        try
        {
            var posts = await http.GetFromJsonAsync<BlogPostMetadata[]>("blog-data/posts-index.json");
            _cachedIndex = posts?
                .OrderByDescending(p => p.Date)
                .ToArray() ?? [];
            logger.LogInformation("Loaded {Count} blog posts", _cachedIndex.Length);
            return _cachedIndex;
        }
        catch (HttpRequestException ex)
        {
            logger.LogWarning(ex, "Blog index not found — content processor may not have run");
            _cachedIndex = [];
            return _cachedIndex;
        }
    }

    public async Task<BlogPostMetadata?> GetPostMetadataAsync(string slug)
    {
        var posts = await GetPostsAsync();
        return posts.FirstOrDefault(p =>
            string.Equals(p.Slug, slug, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
    }

    public async Task<string> GetPostHtmlAsync(string slug)
    {
        logger.LogInformation("Fetching HTML for post: {Slug}", slug);
        try
        {
            return await http.GetStringAsync($"blog-data/{slug}.html");
        }
        catch (HttpRequestException ex)
        {
            logger.LogError(ex, "Failed to load HTML for post: {Slug}", slug);
            return "<p>Could not load post content.</p>";
        }
    }

    public async Task<AuthorProfile[]> GetAllAuthorsAsync()
    {
        if (_cachedAuthors is not null) return _cachedAuthors;

        logger.LogInformation("Fetching authors index");
        try
        {
            var authors = await http.GetFromJsonAsync<AuthorProfile[]>("blog-data/authors.json");
            _cachedAuthors = authors ?? [];
            logger.LogInformation("Loaded {Count} author profiles", _cachedAuthors.Length);
            return _cachedAuthors;
        }
        catch (HttpRequestException ex)
        {
            logger.LogWarning(ex, "Authors index not found");
            _cachedAuthors = [];
            return _cachedAuthors;
        }
    }

    public async Task<AuthorProfile?> GetAuthorAsync(string authorId)
    {
        var authors = await GetAllAuthorsAsync();
        return authors.FirstOrDefault(a =>
            string.Equals(a.Id, authorId, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
    }
}


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Services/BookAnalytics.cs
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
namespace ObserverMagazine.Web.Services;

public sealed class BookAnalytics(IAnalyticsService analytics, ILogger<BookAnalytics> logger) : IBookAnalytics
{
    public static string BookReadersKey(string bookSlug) => $"book--{bookSlug}";

    public static string SubchapterReadersKey(string bookSlug, string chapterSlug, string subchapterSlug) =>
        $"book--{bookSlug}--{chapterSlug}--{subchapterSlug}";

    public Task RecordBookStartedAsync(string bookSlug) =>
        RunSafeAsync(async () =>
        {
            await analytics.TrackInteractionAsync("BookStarted", bookSlug);
            await analytics.IncrementViewAsync(BookReadersKey(bookSlug));
        });

    public Task<int?> GetReaderCountAsync(string bookSlug) =>
        QuerySafeAsync(() => analytics.GetViewCountAsync(BookReadersKey(bookSlug)));

    public Task RecordSubchapterOpenedAsync(string bookSlug, string chapterSlug, string subchapterSlug, bool isFirstVisit) =>
        RunSafeAsync(async () =>
        {
            await analytics.TrackPageViewAsync("BookSubchapter", $"{bookSlug}/{chapterSlug}/{subchapterSlug}");
            if (isFirstVisit)
            {
                await analytics.IncrementViewAsync(SubchapterReadersKey(bookSlug, chapterSlug, subchapterSlug));
            }
        });

    public Task<int?> GetSubchapterReaderCountAsync(string bookSlug, string chapterSlug, string subchapterSlug) =>
        QuerySafeAsync(() => analytics.GetViewCountAsync(SubchapterReadersKey(bookSlug, chapterSlug, subchapterSlug)));

    public Task RecordQuizAnswerAsync(string bookSlug, string chapterSlug, string subchapterSlug, string questionId, bool isCorrect) =>
        RunSafeAsync(() => analytics.TrackInteractionAsync(
            "QuizAnswer",
            $"{bookSlug}/{chapterSlug}/{subchapterSlug}#{questionId}: {(isCorrect ? "correct" : "incorrect")}"));

    private async Task RunSafeAsync(Func<Task> action)
    {
        try
        {
            await action();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            logger.LogDebug(ex, "Book analytics call failed");
        }
    }

    private async Task<int?> QuerySafeAsync(Func<Task<int?>> action)
    {
        try
        {
            return await action();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            logger.LogDebug(ex, "Book analytics query failed");
            return null;
        }
    }
}


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Services/BookNavigator.cs
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
using ObserverMagazine.Web.Models;

namespace ObserverMagazine.Web.Services;

public static class BookNavigator
{
    public static IReadOnlyList<SubchapterLocation> Flatten(BookToc toc) =>
        toc.Chapters
            .SelectMany(chapter => chapter.Subchapters.Select(sub => new SubchapterLocation(chapter, sub)))
            .ToArray();

    public static SubchapterLocation? Find(BookToc toc, string chapterSlug, string subchapterSlug) =>
        Flatten(toc).FirstOrDefault(l =>
            string.Equals(l.Chapter.Slug, chapterSlug, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
            string.Equals(l.Subchapter.Slug, subchapterSlug, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

    public static SubchapterLocation? First(BookToc toc) => Flatten(toc).FirstOrDefault();

    public static SubchapterNeighbors GetNeighbors(BookToc toc, SubchapterLocation current)
    {
        var all = Flatten(toc);
        var index = IndexOf(all, current);
        if (index < 0)
        {
            return new SubchapterNeighbors(null, null);
        }

        return new SubchapterNeighbors(
            index > 0 ? all[index - 1] : null,
            index < all.Count - 1 ? all[index + 1] : null);
    }

    internal static int IndexOf(IReadOnlyList<SubchapterLocation> all, SubchapterLocation location)
    {
        for (var i = 0; i < all.Count; i++)
        {
            if (all[i].ProgressKey == location.ProgressKey)
            {
                return i;
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Services/BookProgressCalculator.cs
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
using ObserverMagazine.Web.Models;

namespace ObserverMagazine.Web.Services;

public static class BookProgressCalculator
{
    public static SubchapterStatus GetStatus(SubchapterLocation location, BookProgress? progress)
    {
        var subProgress = GetSubchapterProgress(location, progress);
        if (subProgress is null || (subProgress.FirstVisitedAt is null && subProgress.Questions.Count == 0))
        {
            return SubchapterStatus.NotStarted;
        }

        return IsComplete(location.Subchapter, subProgress) ? SubchapterStatus.Completed : SubchapterStatus.InProgress;
    }

    public static bool IsComplete(SubchapterToc subchapter, SubchapterProgress? progress)
    {
        if (progress is null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        return subchapter.QuestionIds.Length == 0
            ? progress.FirstVisitedAt is not null
            : subchapter.QuestionIds.All(id => progress.Questions.TryGetValue(id, out var q) && q.AnsweredCorrectly);
    }

    public static int CountCorrect(SubchapterToc subchapter, SubchapterProgress? progress) =>
        progress is null
            ? 0
            : subchapter.QuestionIds.Count(id => progress.Questions.TryGetValue(id, out var q) && q.AnsweredCorrectly);

    public static BookCompletion GetCompletion(BookToc toc, BookProgress? progress)
    {
        var completed = 0;
        var correct = 0;
        var total = 0;
        var totalQuestions = 0;

        foreach (var location in BookNavigator.Flatten(toc))
        {
            var subProgress = GetSubchapterProgress(location, progress);
            total++;
            totalQuestions += location.Subchapter.QuestionIds.Length;
            correct += CountCorrect(location.Subchapter, subProgress);
            if (IsComplete(location.Subchapter, subProgress))
            {
                completed++;
            }
        }

        return new BookCompletion(completed, total, correct, totalQuestions);
    }

    public static SubchapterLocation? GetContinueLocation(BookToc toc, BookProgress? progress)
    {
        var all = BookNavigator.Flatten(toc);
        if (all.Count == 0)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var startIndex = 0;
        if (progress?.LastChapterSlug is { } chapterSlug && progress.LastSubchapterSlug is { } subSlug)
        {
            var last = BookNavigator.Find(toc, chapterSlug, subSlug);
            if (last is not null)
            {
                startIndex = BookNavigator.IndexOf(all, last);
                if (!IsComplete(last.Subchapter, GetSubchapterProgress(last, progress)))
                {
                    return last;
                }
            }
        }

        for (var offset = 0; offset < all.Count; offset++)
        {
            var candidate = all[(startIndex + offset) % all.Count];
            if (!IsComplete(candidate.Subchapter, GetSubchapterProgress(candidate, progress)))
            {
                return candidate;
            }
        }

        return all[0];
    }

    private static SubchapterProgress? GetSubchapterProgress(SubchapterLocation location, BookProgress? progress) =>
        progress?.Subchapters.GetValueOrDefault(location.ProgressKey);
}


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/ObserverMagazine.Web/Services/BookService.cs
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using System.Text.Json;
using ObserverMagazine.Web.Models;

namespace ObserverMagazine.Web.Services;

public sealed class BookService(HttpClient http, ILogger<BookService> logger) : IBookService
{
    public const string DataRoot = "book-data";

    private readonly Dictionary<string, BookToc> _tocCache = [with(StringComparer.Ordinal)];
    private BookSummary[]? _indexCache;

    public async Task<BookSummary[]> GetBooksAsync()
    {
        if (_indexCache is not null)
        {
            return _indexCache;
        }

        var books = await FetchAsync<BookSummary[]>($"{DataRoot}/books-index.json");
        if (books is null)
        {
            return [];
        }

        _indexCache = books.OrderByDescending(b => b.Date).ToArray();
        logger.LogInformation("Loaded {Count} books", _indexCache.Length);
        return _indexCache;
    }

    public async Task<BookSummary?> GetBookAsync(string bookSlug)
    {
        if (!ContentSlug.TryNormalize(bookSlug, out var slug))
        {
            return null;
        }

        var books = await GetBooksAsync();
        return books.FirstOrDefault(b => string.Equals(b.Slug, slug, StringComparison.Ordinal));
    }

    public async Task<BookToc?> GetTocAsync(string bookSlug)
    {
        if (!ContentSlug.TryNormalize(bookSlug, out var slug))
        {
            return null;
        }

        if (_tocCache.TryGetValue(slug, out var cached))
        {
            return cached;
        }

        var toc = await FetchAsync<BookToc>($"{DataRoot}/{slug}/toc.json");
        if (toc is not null)
        {
            _tocCache[slug] = toc;
        }
        return toc;
    }

    public async Task<SubchapterContent?> GetSubchapterAsync(string bookSlug, string chapterSlug, string subchapterSlug)
    {
        if (!ContentSlug.TryNormalize(bookSlug, out var book) ||
            !ContentSlug.TryNormalize(chapterSlug, out var chapter) ||
            !ContentSlug.TryNormalize(subchapterSlug, out var subchapter))
        {
            return null;
        }

        return await FetchAsync<SubchapterContent>($"{DataRoot}/{book}/{chapter}/{subchapter}.json");
    }

    private async Task<T?> FetchAsync<T>(string path) where T : class
    {
        try
        {
            return await http.GetFromJsonAsync<T>(path);
        }
        catch (HttpRequestException ex)
        {
            logger.LogWarning(ex, "Book data not available at {Path}", path);
        }
        catch (JsonException ex)
        {
            logger.LogError(ex, "Book data at {Path} is not valid JSON", path);
        }
        catch (NotSupportedException ex)
        {
            logger.LogError(ex, "Book data at {Path} has an unsupported content type", path);
        }
        catch (TaskCanceledException ex)
        {
            logger.LogWarning(ex, "Timed out loading book data at {Path}", path);
        }
        return null;
    }
}
