using System.Globalization;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor.Books;

public static partial class ContentNaming
{
    [GeneratedRegex(@"^(\d+)[-_]([a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*)$", RegexOptions.CultureInvariant)]
    private static partial Regex NumberedPattern();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$", RegexOptions.CultureInvariant)]
    private static partial Regex SlugPattern();

    public static bool IsValidSlug(string? slug) =>
        !string.IsNullOrEmpty(slug) && SlugPattern().IsMatch(slug);

    public static bool TryParseNumbered(string name, out int order, out string slug)
    {
        order = 0;
        slug = "";

        var match = NumberedPattern().Match(name);
        if (!match.Success ||
            !int.TryParse(match.Groups[1].ValueSpan, NumberStyles.None, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out order))
        {
            return false;
        }

        slug = match.Groups[2].Value;
        return true;
    }

    public static bool IsHidden(string path)
    {
        var name = Path.GetFileName(path.TrimEnd(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar, Path.AltDirectorySeparatorChar));
        return name.StartsWith('.') || name.StartsWith('_');
    }
}


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: tools/ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor/Books/QuestionCompiler.cs
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
namespace ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor.Books;

public static class QuestionCompiler
{
    public const string SingleType = "single";
    public const string MultipleType = "multiple";
    private const int MinimumOptions = 2;

    public static IReadOnlyList<QuestionDocument> Compile(
        IReadOnlyList<QuestionDefinition>? definitions,
        string location,
        BuildDiagnostics diagnostics)
    {
        if (definitions is null || definitions.Count == 0)
        {
            return [];
        }

        List<QuestionDocument> questions = [with(capacity: definitions.Count)];
        HashSet<string> seenIds = [with(StringComparer.Ordinal)];

        for (var i = 0; i < definitions.Count; i++)
        {
            var definition = definitions[i];
            var id = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(definition.Id) ? $"q{i + 1}" : definition.Id.Trim();
            var questionLocation = $"{location} question '{id}'";

            if (!ContentNaming.IsValidSlug(id))
            {
                diagnostics.Error(questionLocation, "id must contain only lowercase letters, digits and single hyphens");
                continue;
            }

            if (!seenIds.Add(id))
            {
                diagnostics.Error(questionLocation, "duplicate question id");
                continue;
            }

            var question = CompileQuestion(definition, id, questionLocation, diagnostics);
            if (question is not null)
            {
                questions.Add(question);
            }
        }

        return questions;
    }

    private static QuestionDocument? CompileQuestion(
        QuestionDefinition definition,
        string id,
        string location,
        BuildDiagnostics diagnostics)
    {
        var errorCount = diagnostics.Errors.Count;

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(definition.Prompt))
        {
            diagnostics.Error(location, "prompt is required");
        }

        var options = CompileOptions(definition.Options ?? [], location, diagnostics);
        var correctCount = options.Count(o => o.Correct);

        if (options.Count < MinimumOptions)
        {
            diagnostics.Error(location, $"at least {MinimumOptions} options are required");
        }

        if (correctCount == 0)
        {
            diagnostics.Error(location, "at least one option must be marked correct");
        }

        var multipleAnswers = ResolveMultipleAnswers(definition.Type, correctCount, location, diagnostics);

        if (diagnostics.Errors.Count > errorCount)
        {
            return null;
        }

        return new QuestionDocument
        {
            Id = id,
            PromptHtml = MarkdownProcessor.ToHtml(definition.Prompt.Trim()),
            MultipleAnswers = multipleAnswers,
            ExplanationHtml = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(definition.Explanation)
                ? ""
                : MarkdownProcessor.ToHtml(definition.Explanation.Trim()),
            Options = options
        };
    }

    private static List<OptionDocument> CompileOptions(
        IReadOnlyList<OptionDefinition> definitions,
        string location,
        BuildDiagnostics diagnostics)
    {
        List<OptionDocument> options = [with(capacity: definitions.Count)];
        HashSet<string> seenIds = [with(StringComparer.Ordinal)];

        for (var i = 0; i < definitions.Count; i++)
        {
            var definition = definitions[i];
            var id = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(definition.Id) ? DefaultOptionId(i) : definition.Id.Trim();

            if (!ContentNaming.IsValidSlug(id))
            {
                diagnostics.Error(location, $"option id '{id}' must contain only lowercase letters, digits and single hyphens");
                continue;
            }

            if (!seenIds.Add(id))
            {
                diagnostics.Error(location, $"duplicate option id '{id}'");
                continue;
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(definition.Text))
            {
                diagnostics.Error(location, $"option '{id}' has no text");
                continue;
            }

            options.Add(new OptionDocument
            {
                Id = id,
                TextHtml = MarkdownProcessor.ToInlineHtml(definition.Text.Trim()),
                Correct = definition.Correct
            });
        }

        return options;
    }

    private static bool ResolveMultipleAnswers(
        string? type,
        int correctCount,
        string location,
        BuildDiagnostics diagnostics)
    {
        var normalized = type?.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
        switch (normalized)
        {
            case null or "":
                return correctCount > 1;
            case MultipleType:
                return true;
            case SingleType:
                if (correctCount > 1)
                {
                    diagnostics.Error(location, "type is 'single' but more than one option is marked correct");
                }
                return false;
            default:
                diagnostics.Error(location, $"unknown type '{type}' (expected '{SingleType}' or '{MultipleType}')");
                return false;
        }
    }

    public static string DefaultOptionId(int index) =>
        index < 26 ? ((char)('a' + index)).ToString() : $"o{index + 1}";
}


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: tools/ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor/FrontMatterParser.cs
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
using YamlDotNet.Serialization;
using YamlDotNet.Serialization.NamingConventions;

namespace ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor;

public static class FrontMatterParser
{
    private const string Delimiter = "---";
    private const string ClosingDelimiter = "\n---";

    private static readonly IDeserializer Deserializer = new DeserializerBuilder()
        .WithNamingConvention(CamelCaseNamingConvention.Instance)
        .IgnoreUnmatchedProperties()
        .Build();

    public static (FrontMatter FrontMatter, string MarkdownBody) Parse(string rawContent) =>
        Parse<FrontMatter>(rawContent);

    public static (T FrontMatter, string MarkdownBody) Parse<T>(string rawContent) where T : new()
    {
        if (!rawContent.StartsWith(Delimiter, StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            return (new T(), rawContent);
        }

        var endIndex = rawContent.IndexOf(ClosingDelimiter, Delimiter.Length, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        if (endIndex < 0)
        {
            return (new T(), rawContent);
        }

        var yamlBlock = rawContent[Delimiter.Length..endIndex].Trim();
        var body = rawContent[(endIndex + ClosingDelimiter.Length)..].TrimStart('\r', '\n');

        return (DeserializeYaml<T>(yamlBlock), body);
    }

    public static T DeserializeYaml<T>(string yaml) where T : new() =>
        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(yaml) ? new T() : Deserializer.Deserialize<T>(yaml) ?? new T();

    public static AuthorProfile? ParseAuthor(string yamlContent, string id)
    {
        try
        {
            var profile = Deserializer.Deserialize<AuthorProfile>(yamlContent);
            profile.Id = id;
            return profile;
        }
        catch
        {
            return null;
        }
    }

    public static string DeriveSlug(string fileName)
    {
        if (fileName.Length > 11 &&
            char.IsDigit(fileName[0]) &&
            fileName[4] == '-' &&
            fileName[7] == '-' &&
            fileName[10] == '-')
        {
            return fileName[11..];
        }
        return fileName;
    }

    public static int CalculateReadingTime(string markdownBody)
    {
        var wordCount = markdownBody
            .Split([' ', '\n', '\r', '\t'], StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)
            .Length;
        return Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Ceiling(wordCount / 200.0));
    }
}

public sealed class FrontMatter
{
    public string Title { get; init; } = "";
    public DateTime Date { get; init; }
    public DateTime? Updated { get; init; }
    public string? Author { get; init; }
    public string? Summary { get; init; }
    public string[]? Tags { get; init; }
    public bool Draft { get; init; }
    public bool Featured { get; init; }
    public string? Series { get; init; }
    public string? Image { get; init; }
}

public sealed class AuthorProfile
{
    public string Id { get; set; } = "";
    public string Name { get; init; } = "";
    public string? Email { get; init; }
    public string? Bio { get; init; }
    public string? Avatar { get; init; }
    public Dictionary<string, string>? Socials { get; init; }
}


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: tools/ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor/MarkdownProcessor.cs
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
using Markdig;

namespace ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor;

public static class MarkdownProcessor
{
    private const string ParagraphOpen = "<p>";
    private const string ParagraphClose = "</p>";

    private static readonly MarkdownPipeline Pipeline = new MarkdownPipelineBuilder()
        .UseAdvancedExtensions()
        .Use<SyntaxHighlightingExtension>()
        .Build();

    public static string ToHtml(string markdown) => Markdown.ToHtml(markdown, Pipeline);

    public static string ToInlineHtml(string markdown)
    {
        var html = ToHtml(markdown).Trim();
        var isSingleParagraph =
            html.StartsWith(ParagraphOpen, StringComparison.Ordinal) &&
            html.EndsWith(ParagraphClose, StringComparison.Ordinal) &&
            html.IndexOf(ParagraphOpen, ParagraphOpen.Length, StringComparison.Ordinal) < 0;

        return isSingleParagraph
            ? html[ParagraphOpen.Length..^ParagraphClose.Length]
            : html;
    }
}

FILE: tools/ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor/Program.cs
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
using System.Text.Json;
using ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor;
using ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor.Books;

string contentDir = "content/blog";
string outputDir = "src/ObserverMagazine.Web/wwwroot";
string authorsDir = "content/authors";
string booksDir = "content/books";
DateTime publishBefore = DateTime.UtcNow;

for (int i = 0; i < args.Length; i++)
{
    if (args[i] == "--content-dir" && i + 1 < args.Length)
        contentDir = args[++i];
    else if (args[i] == "--output-dir" && i + 1 < args.Length)
        outputDir = args[++i];
    else if (args[i] == "--authors-dir" && i + 1 < args.Length)
        authorsDir = args[++i];
    else if (args[i] == "--books-dir" && i + 1 < args.Length)
        booksDir = args[++i];
    else if (args[i] == "--publish-before" && i + 1 < args.Length)
        publishBefore = DateTime.Parse(args[++i]);
}

Console.WriteLine($"Content directory: {contentDir}");
Console.WriteLine($"Output directory:  {outputDir}");
Console.WriteLine($"Authors directory: {authorsDir}");
Console.WriteLine($"Books directory:   {booksDir}");
Console.WriteLine($"Publish before:    {publishBefore:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss} UTC");

Dictionary<string, AuthorProfile> authorProfiles = [with(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)];

if (Directory.Exists(authorsDir))
{
    var authorFiles = Directory.GetFiles(authorsDir, "*.yml", SearchOption.TopDirectoryOnly);
    Console.WriteLine($"Found {authorFiles.Length} author profile(s)");

    foreach (var authorFile in authorFiles)
    {
        var authorId = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(authorFile);
        var yamlContent = File.ReadAllText(authorFile);
        var profile = FrontMatterParser.ParseAuthor(yamlContent, authorId);

        if (profile is not null)
        {
            authorProfiles[authorId] = profile;
            Console.WriteLine($"  Loaded author: {authorId} ({profile.Name})");
        }
        else
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"  WARNING: Could not parse author file {authorFile}");
        }
    }
}
else
{
    Console.WriteLine($"Authors directory '{authorsDir}' does not exist. No author profiles loaded.");
}

string blogDataDir = Path.Combine(outputDir, "blog-data");
Directory.CreateDirectory(blogDataDir);

var jsonOptions = new JsonSerializerOptions
{
    PropertyNamingPolicy = JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase,
    WriteIndented = true
};

var authorsPath = Path.Combine(blogDataDir, "authors.json");
var authorsJson = JsonSerializer.Serialize(authorProfiles.Values.ToArray(), jsonOptions);
File.WriteAllText(authorsPath, authorsJson);
Console.WriteLine($"Wrote authors index: {authorsPath} ({authorProfiles.Count} authors)");

if (!Directory.Exists(contentDir))
{
    Console.WriteLine($"Content directory '{contentDir}' does not exist. Creating with no posts.");
    Directory.CreateDirectory(contentDir);
}

var markdownFiles = Directory.GetFiles(contentDir, "*.md", SearchOption.TopDirectoryOnly);
Console.WriteLine($"Found {markdownFiles.Length} markdown files");

List<PostIndexEntry> allPostMetadata = [];
Dictionary<string, string> postHtmlMap = [];
int skippedDrafts = 0;
int skippedFuture = 0;

foreach (var mdFile in markdownFiles)
{
    Console.WriteLine($"Processing: {Path.GetFileName(mdFile)}");

    var rawContent = File.ReadAllText(mdFile);
    var (frontMatter, markdownBody) = FrontMatterParser.Parse(rawContent);

    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(frontMatter.Title))
    {
        Console.WriteLine($"  WARNING: No title in front matter, skipping {mdFile}");
        continue;
    }

    if (frontMatter.Draft)
    {
        Console.WriteLine($"  SKIPPED: Draft post '{frontMatter.Title}'");
        skippedDrafts++;
        continue;
    }

    if (frontMatter.Date > publishBefore)
    {
        Console.WriteLine($"  SKIPPED: Future post '{frontMatter.Title}' (date: {frontMatter.Date:yyyy-MM-dd}, publish-before: {publishBefore:yyyy-MM-dd})");
        skippedFuture++;
        continue;
    }

    var fileName = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(mdFile);
    var slug = FrontMatterParser.DeriveSlug(fileName);

    var html = MarkdownProcessor.ToHtml(markdownBody);
    var readingTime = FrontMatterParser.CalculateReadingTime(markdownBody);

    var authorId = frontMatter.Author ?? "";
    var authorName = authorId;
    string? authorEmail = null;
    if (authorProfiles.TryGetValue(authorId, out var authorProfile))
    {
        authorName = authorProfile.Name;
        authorEmail = authorProfile.Email;
    }
    else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(authorId))
    {
        Console.WriteLine($"  WARNING: No author profile found for '{authorId}'");
    }

    var htmlPath = Path.Combine(blogDataDir, $"{slug}.html");
    File.WriteAllText(htmlPath, html);
    Console.WriteLine($"  Wrote: {htmlPath} (~{readingTime} min read)");

    postHtmlMap[slug] = html;

    allPostMetadata.Add(new PostIndexEntry
    {
        Slug = slug,
        Title = frontMatter.Title,
        Date = frontMatter.Date,
        Updated = frontMatter.Updated,
        Author = authorId,
        AuthorName = authorName,
        AuthorEmail = authorEmail,
        Summary = frontMatter.Summary ?? "",
        Tags = frontMatter.Tags ?? [],
        Featured = frontMatter.Featured,
        Series = frontMatter.Series,
        Image = frontMatter.Image,
        ReadingTimeMinutes = readingTime
    });
}

allPostMetadata.Sort((a, b) => b.Date.CompareTo(a.Date));

var indexPath = Path.Combine(blogDataDir, "posts-index.json");
var indexJson = JsonSerializer.Serialize(allPostMetadata, jsonOptions);
File.WriteAllText(indexPath, indexJson);
Console.WriteLine($"Wrote posts index: {indexPath} ({allPostMetadata.Count} posts, {skippedDrafts} drafts skipped, {skippedFuture} future posts skipped)");

var feedPath = Path.Combine(outputDir, "feed.xml");
var rssXml = RssGenerator.Generate(
    title: "Terlingua Magazine",
    description: "A free, open-source Blazor WebAssembly showcase on .NET 11",
    siteUrl: "https://terlingua.github.io",
    posts: allPostMetadata,
    getPostHtml: slug => postHtmlMap.GetValueOrDefault(slug)
);
File.WriteAllText(feedPath, rssXml);
Console.WriteLine($"Wrote RSS feed: {feedPath}");

var bookDiagnostics = new BuildDiagnostics();
var books = new BookCompiler(authorProfiles, publishBefore, bookDiagnostics, Console.Out).Compile(booksDir);

foreach (var warning in bookDiagnostics.Warnings)
{
    Console.WriteLine($"  WARNING: {warning}");
}

if (bookDiagnostics.HasErrors)
{
    foreach (var error in bookDiagnostics.Errors)
    {
        Console.Error.WriteLine($"  ERROR: {error}");
    }
    Console.Error.WriteLine($"Book processing failed with {bookDiagnostics.Errors.Count} error(s).");
    return 1;
}

var bookDataDir = BookOutputWriter.Write(books, outputDir, jsonOptions);
Console.WriteLine($"Wrote book data: {bookDataDir} ({books.Count} books)");

Console.WriteLine("Content processing complete.");
return 0;

public sealed class PostIndexEntry
{
    public string Slug { get; init; } = "";
    public string Title { get; init; } = "";
    public DateTime Date { get; init; }
    public DateTime? Updated { get; init; }
    public string Author { get; init; } = "";
    public string AuthorName { get; init; } = "";
    public string? AuthorEmail { get; init; }
    public string Summary { get; init; } = "";
    public string[] Tags { get; init; } = [];
    public bool Featured { get; init; }
    public string? Series { get; init; }
    public string? Image { get; init; }
    public int ReadingTimeMinutes { get; init; }
}

FILE: tools/ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor/RssGenerator.cs
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
using System.Xml.Linq;

namespace ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor;

public static class RssGenerator
{
    private static readonly XNamespace ContentNs = "http://purl.org/rss/1.0/modules/content/";

    public static string Generate(
        string title, string description, string siteUrl,
        IReadOnlyList<PostIndexEntry> posts,
        Func<string, string?>? getPostHtml = null)
    {
        var items = posts.Select(p =>
        {
            List<object> itemElements =
            [
                new XElement("title", p.Title),
                new XElement("link", $"{siteUrl}/blog/{p.Slug}"),
                new XElement("description", p.Summary),
                new XElement("pubDate", p.Date.ToString("R")),
                new XElement("guid", $"{siteUrl}/blog/{p.Slug}")
            ];

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(p.AuthorEmail))
            {
                itemElements.Add(new XElement("author", $"{p.AuthorEmail} ({p.AuthorName})"));
            }

            var html = getPostHtml?.Invoke(p.Slug);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(html))
            {
                itemElements.Add(new XElement(ContentNs + "encoded", new XCData(html)));
            }

            if (p.Tags.Length > 0)
            {
                itemElements.AddRange(p.Tags.Select(t => new XElement("category", t)));
            }

            return new XElement("item", itemElements);
        });

        var rss = new XDocument(
            new XDeclaration("1.0", "utf-8", null),
            new XElement("rss",
                new XAttribute("version", "2.0"),
                new XAttribute(XNamespace.Xmlns + "content", ContentNs),
                new XElement("channel",
                    new XElement("title", title),
                    new XElement("link", siteUrl),
                    new XElement("description", description),
                    new XElement("language", "en-us"),
                    new XElement("lastBuildDate", DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("R")),
                    items
                )
            )
        );

        return rss.Declaration + Environment.NewLine + rss;
    }
}


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: tools/ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor/SyntaxHighlighter.cs
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
using System.Collections.Frozen;
using System.Text;

namespace ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor;

public static class SyntaxHighlighter
{
    private static readonly FrozenDictionary<string, string> Aliases = new Dictionary<string, string>
    {
        ["cs"] = "csharp",
        ["ts"] = "typescript",
        ["js"] = "javascript",
        ["sh"] = "bash",
        ["shell"] = "bash",
        ["zsh"] = "bash",
        ["yml"] = "yaml",
        ["xml"] = "html"
    }.ToFrozenDictionary();

    private static readonly FrozenDictionary<string, FrozenSet<string>> Keywords = new Dictionary<string, FrozenSet<string>>
    {
        ["csharp"] = Set("abstract as async await base bool break byte case catch char checked class closed const continue decimal default delegate do double else enum event explicit extension extern false field finally fixed float for foreach goto if implicit in init int interface internal is lock long namespace new null object operator out override params partial private protected public readonly record ref required return sbyte scoped sealed short sizeof stackalloc static string struct switch this throw true try typeof uint ulong unchecked union unsafe ushort using var virtual void volatile when where while with yield and or not global file nint nuint nameof value"),
        ["javascript"] = Set("async await break case catch class const continue debugger default delete do else export extends false finally for from function if import in instanceof let new null of return static super switch this throw true try typeof undefined var void while with yield"),
        ["typescript"] = Set("abstract as async await break case catch class const continue debugger declare default delete do else enum export extends false finally for from function if implements import in instanceof interface keyof let module namespace new null of override readonly return satisfies static super switch this throw true try type typeof undefined var void while with yield infer never unknown any string number boolean symbol bigint"),
        ["python"] = Set("False None True and as assert async await break class continue def del elif else except finally for from global if import in is lambda nonlocal not or pass raise return try while with yield self print"),
        ["sql"] = Set("SELECT FROM WHERE INSERT UPDATE DELETE CREATE ALTER DROP TABLE INDEX VIEW JOIN INNER LEFT RIGHT OUTER CROSS ON AND OR NOT NULL IS IN LIKE BETWEEN EXISTS HAVING GROUP BY ORDER ASC DESC LIMIT OFFSET UNION ALL AS SET INTO VALUES DISTINCT COUNT SUM AVG MIN MAX CASE WHEN THEN ELSE END BEGIN COMMIT ROLLBACK TRANSACTION PRIMARY KEY FOREIGN REFERENCES CONSTRAINT DEFAULT CHECK UNIQUE TOP GO USE EXEC DECLARE NVARCHAR VARCHAR INT BIT DATETIME IDENTITY NOLOCK WITH", StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase),
        ["bash"] = Set("if then else elif fi for while do done case esac function return in select until echo exit export source alias set unset readonly local declare eval exec trap cd ls grep awk sed cat mkdir rm cp mv chmod chown sudo apt dnf pip npm dotnet git docker podman")
    }.ToFrozenDictionary();

    private static readonly FrozenSet<string> YamlLiterals = Set("true false yes no on off null ~", StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    public static string Highlight(string code, string? language)
    {
        var lang = (language ?? "").Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
        if (lang.StartsWith("language-", StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            lang = lang["language-".Length..];
        }
        lang = Aliases.GetValueOrDefault(lang, lang);

        return lang switch
        {
            "json" => HighlightJson(code),
            "yaml" => HighlightYaml(code),
            "html" => HighlightHtml(code),
            "css" => HighlightCss(code),
            _ => HighlightGeneral(code, Keywords.ContainsKey(lang) ? lang : "")
        };
    }

    private static FrozenSet<string> Set(string words, StringComparer? comparer = null) =>
        words.Split(' ', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).ToFrozenSet(comparer ?? StringComparer.Ordinal);

    private static bool IsAsciiLetter(char c) => char.IsAsciiLetter(c);
    private static bool IsIdentStart(char c) => char.IsAsciiLetter(c) || c is '_' or '$';
    private static bool IsIdentPart(char c) => char.IsAsciiLetterOrDigit(c) || c is '_' or '$';
    private static bool IsWordChar(char c) => char.IsAsciiLetterOrDigit(c) || c == '_';

    private static char At(string s, int index) => index >= 0 && index < s.Length ? s[index] : '\0';

    private static void Escape(StringBuilder sb, ReadOnlySpan<char> text)
    {
        foreach (var c in text)
        {
            switch (c)
            {
                case '&': sb.Append("&amp;"); break;
                case '<': sb.Append("&lt;"); break;
                case '>': sb.Append("&gt;"); break;
                default: sb.Append(c); break;
            }
        }
    }

    private static void Wrap(StringBuilder sb, string cls, ReadOnlySpan<char> text)
    {
        sb.Append("<span class=\"hl-").Append(cls).Append("\">");
        Escape(sb, text);
        sb.Append("</span>");
    }

    private static int EndOf(string code, string marker, int from, int extra)
    {
        var found = code.IndexOf(marker, from, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        return found < 0 ? code.Length : found + extra;
    }

    private static string HighlightGeneral(string code, string lang)
    {
        var keywords = Keywords.GetValueOrDefault(lang);
        var hashComments = lang is "python" or "bash";
        var dashComments = lang == "sql";
        var n = code.Length;
        var sb = new StringBuilder(code.Length * 2);
        var i = 0;

        while (i < n)
        {
            var ch = code[i];

            if ((ch == '/' && At(code, i + 1) == '/') ||
                (hashComments && ch == '#' && (i == 0 || char.IsWhiteSpace(code[i - 1]))) ||
                (dashComments && ch == '-' && At(code, i + 1) == '-'))
            {
                var end = code.IndexOf('\n', i);
                if (end < 0) end = n;
                Wrap(sb, "comment", code.AsSpan(i, end - i));
                i = end;
                continue;
            }

            if (ch == '/' && At(code, i + 1) == '*')
            {
                var end = EndOf(code, "*/", i + 2, 2);
                Wrap(sb, "comment", code.AsSpan(i, end - i));
                i = end;
                continue;
            }

            if (ch is '"' or '\'' or '`')
            {
                if (At(code, i + 1) == ch && At(code, i + 2) == ch)
                {
                    var end = EndOf(code, new string(ch, 3), i + 3, 3);
                    Wrap(sb, "string", code.AsSpan(i, end - i));
                    i = end;
                    continue;
                }

                var j = i + 1;
                while (j < n)
                {
                    if (code[j] == '\\') { j += 2; continue; }
                    if (code[j] == ch) { j++; break; }
                    if (ch != '`' && code[j] == '\n') break;
                    j++;
                }
                j = Math.Min(j, n);
                Wrap(sb, "string", code.AsSpan(i, j - i));
                i = j;
                continue;
            }

            if (lang == "csharp" && ch is '@' or '$')
            {
                var next = At(code, i + 1);
                var start = i;
                var prefix = next == '"' ? 2 : (next is '@' or '$') && At(code, i + 2) == '"' ? 3 : 0;
                if (prefix == 0)
                {
                    i = Identifier(code, i, keywords, sb);
                    continue;
                }

                var j = i + prefix;
                while (j < n)
                {
                    if (code[j] == '"')
                    {
                        if (At(code, j + 1) == '"') { j += 2; continue; }
                        j++;
                        break;
                    }
                    j++;
                }
                j = Math.Min(j, n);
                Wrap(sb, "string", code.AsSpan(start, j - start));
                i = j;
                continue;
            }

            if (char.IsAsciiDigit(ch) && (i == 0 || !IsIdentStart(code[i - 1])))
            {
                var j = i;
                if (ch == '0' && At(code, j + 1) is 'x' or 'X' or 'b' or 'B' or 'o' or 'O') j += 2;
                while (j < n && (char.IsAsciiHexDigit(code[j]) || code[j] is '.' or '_' or 'u' or 'U' or 'l' or 'L' or 'm' or 'M' or 'n' or 'N')) j++;
                j = Math.Min(j, n);
                Wrap(sb, "number", code.AsSpan(i, j - i));
                i = j;
                continue;
            }

            if (IsIdentStart(ch))
            {
                i = Identifier(code, i, keywords, sb);
                continue;
            }

            Escape(sb, [ch]);
            i++;
        }

        return sb.ToString();
    }

    private static int Identifier(string code, int i, FrozenSet<string>? keywords, StringBuilder sb)
    {
        var n = code.Length;
        var j = i;
        while (j < n && IsIdentPart(code[j])) j++;
        if (j == i)
        {
            Escape(sb, [code[i]]);
            return i + 1;
        }

        var word = code[i..j];
        if (keywords?.Contains(word) == true)
        {
            Wrap(sb, "keyword", word);
        }
        else if (word.Length > 1 && char.IsAsciiLetterUpper(word[0]) && word.All(char.IsAsciiLetterOrDigit) && word is not ("False" or "True" or "None"))
        {
            Wrap(sb, "type", word);
        }
        else
        {
            var k = j;
            while (k < n && code[k] == ' ') k++;
            if (At(code, k) == '(')
            {
                Wrap(sb, "function", word);
            }
            else
            {
                Escape(sb, word);
            }
        }

        return j;
    }

    private static string HighlightJson(string code)
    {
        var n = code.Length;
        var sb = new StringBuilder(code.Length * 2);
        var i = 0;

        while (i < n)
        {
            var ch = code[i];
            if (ch == '"')
            {
                var j = i + 1;
                while (j < n && code[j] != '"')
                {
                    if (code[j] == '\\') j++;
                    j++;
                }
                if (j < n) j++;
                j = Math.Min(j, n);
                var k = j;
                while (k < n && char.IsWhiteSpace(code[k])) k++;
                Wrap(sb, At(code, k) == ':' ? "attr" : "string", code.AsSpan(i, j - i));
                i = j;
                continue;
            }

            if (char.IsAsciiDigit(ch) || (ch == '-' && char.IsAsciiDigit(At(code, i + 1))))
            {
                var j = i;
                if (code[j] == '-') j++;
                while (j < n && (char.IsAsciiDigit(code[j]) || code[j] is '.' or 'e' or 'E' or '+' or '-')) j++;
                Wrap(sb, "number", code.AsSpan(i, j - i));
                i = j;
                continue;
            }

            if (IsAsciiLetter(ch))
            {
                var j = i;
                while (j < n && IsAsciiLetter(code[j])) j++;
                var word = code.AsSpan(i, j - i);
                if (word is "true" or "false" or "null")
                {
                    Wrap(sb, "literal", word);
                }
                else
                {
                    Escape(sb, word);
                }
                i = j;
                continue;
            }

            Escape(sb, [ch]);
            i++;
        }

        return sb.ToString();
    }

    private static string HighlightYaml(string code)
    {
        var sb = new StringBuilder(code.Length * 2);
        var lines = code.Split('\n');

        for (var index = 0; index < lines.Length; index++)
        {
            if (index > 0) sb.Append('\n');
            var line = lines[index];

            if (line.TrimStart().StartsWith('#'))
            {
                Wrap(sb, "comment", line);
                continue;
            }

            var indent = 0;
            while (indent < line.Length && char.IsWhiteSpace(line[indent])) indent++;

            var keyEnd = indent;
            if (keyEnd < line.Length && IsWordChar(line[keyEnd]))
            {
                keyEnd++;
                while (keyEnd < line.Length && (IsWordChar(line[keyEnd]) || line[keyEnd] is '.' or '-')) keyEnd++;
            }

            var colon = keyEnd;
            while (colon < line.Length && char.IsWhiteSpace(line[colon])) colon++;

            if (keyEnd == indent || colon >= line.Length || line[colon] != ':')
            {
                Escape(sb, line);
                continue;
            }

            Escape(sb, line.AsSpan(0, indent));
            Wrap(sb, "attr", line.AsSpan(indent, colon - indent));
            sb.Append(':');

            var value = line[(colon + 1)..].Trim();
            if (value.Length == 0) continue;

            sb.Append(' ');
            if (YamlLiterals.Contains(value))
            {
                Wrap(sb, "literal", value);
            }
            else if (char.IsAsciiDigit(value[0]) || (value[0] == '-' && value.Length > 1 && char.IsAsciiDigit(value[1])))
            {
                Wrap(sb, "number", value);
            }
            else if (value[0] is '"' or '\'')
            {
                Wrap(sb, "string", value);
            }
            else
            {
                Escape(sb, value);
            }
        }

        return sb.ToString();
    }

    private static string HighlightHtml(string code)
    {
        var n = code.Length;
        var sb = new StringBuilder(code.Length * 2);
        var i = 0;

        while (i < n)
        {
            var ch = code[i];

            if (ch == '<' && code.AsSpan(i).StartsWith("<!--"))
            {
                var end = EndOf(code, "-->", i + 4, 3);
                Wrap(sb, "comment", code.AsSpan(i, end - i));
                i = end;
                continue;
            }

            if (ch == '<' && code.AsSpan(i).StartsWith("<![CDATA["))
            {
                var end = EndOf(code, "]]>", i + 9, 3);
                Wrap(sb, "comment", code.AsSpan(i, end - i));
                i = end;
                continue;
            }

            if (ch == '<' && At(code, i + 1) is '!' or '?')
            {
                var end = EndOf(code, ">", i, 1);
                Wrap(sb, "comment", code.AsSpan(i, end - i));
                i = end;
                continue;
            }

            if (ch == '<' && i + 1 < n && (IsAsciiLetter(code[i + 1]) || code[i + 1] == '/'))
            {
                sb.Append("&lt;");
                i++;

                if (code[i] == '/')
                {
                    sb.Append('/');
                    i++;
                }

                var nameStart = i;
                while (i < n && (IsWordChar(code[i]) || code[i] is ':' or '-')) i++;
                if (i > nameStart) Wrap(sb, "tag", code.AsSpan(nameStart, i - nameStart));

                while (i < n && code[i] != '>')
                {
                    if (char.IsWhiteSpace(code[i]) || code[i] == '/')
                    {
                        sb.Append(code[i]);
                        i++;
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (IsWordChar(code[i]) || code[i] is ':' or '-')
                    {
                        var attrStart = i;
                        while (i < n && (IsWordChar(code[i]) || code[i] is ':' or '.' or '-')) i++;
                        Wrap(sb, "attr", code.AsSpan(attrStart, i - attrStart));

                        var look = i;
                        while (look < n && char.IsWhiteSpace(code[look])) look++;
                        if (At(code, look) == '=')
                        {
                            sb.Append(code.AsSpan(i, look - i)).Append('=');
                            i = look + 1;
                            while (i < n && char.IsWhiteSpace(code[i]))
                            {
                                sb.Append(code[i]);
                                i++;
                            }

                            var valueStart = i;
                            if (At(code, i) is '"' or '\'')
                            {
                                var quote = code[i];
                                i++;
                                while (i < n && code[i] != quote) i++;
                                if (i < n) i++;
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                while (i < n && !char.IsWhiteSpace(code[i]) && code[i] is not ('>' or '/')) i++;
                            }

                            if (i > valueStart) Wrap(sb, "string", code.AsSpan(valueStart, i - valueStart));
                        }
                        continue;
                    }

                    Escape(sb, [code[i]]);
                    i++;
                }

                if (i < n)
                {
                    sb.Append("&gt;");
                    i++;
                }
                continue;
            }

            Escape(sb, [ch]);
            i++;
        }

        return sb.ToString();
    }

    private static string HighlightCss(string code)
    {
        var n = code.Length;
        var sb = new StringBuilder(code.Length * 2);
        var i = 0;

        while (i < n)
        {
            var ch = code[i];

            if (ch == '/' && At(code, i + 1) == '*')
            {
                var end = EndOf(code, "*/", i + 2, 2);
                Wrap(sb, "comment", code.AsSpan(i, end - i));
                i = end;
                continue;
            }

            if (ch is '"' or '\'')
            {
                var j = i + 1;
                while (j < n && code[j] != ch)
                {
                    if (code[j] == '\\') j++;
                    j++;
                }
                if (j < n) j++;
                j = Math.Min(j, n);
                Wrap(sb, "string", code.AsSpan(i, j - i));
                i = j;
                continue;
            }

            if (char.IsAsciiDigit(ch) && (i == 0 || !(IsAsciiLetter(code[i - 1]) || code[i - 1] is '_' or '-')))
            {
                var j = i;
                while (j < n && (char.IsAsciiLetterOrDigit(code[j]) || code[j] is '.' or '%')) j++;
                Wrap(sb, "number", code.AsSpan(i, j - i));
                i = j;
                continue;
            }

            if (ch == '#' && char.IsAsciiHexDigit(At(code, i + 1)))
            {
                var j = i + 1;
                while (j < n && char.IsAsciiHexDigit(code[j])) j++;
                Wrap(sb, "number", code.AsSpan(i, j - i));
                i = j;
                continue;
            }

            if (ch is '.' or '#' or '@' or ':' or '[' || (IsAsciiLetter(ch) && (i == 0 || code[i - 1] is '\n' or '}')))
            {
                var j = i;
                while (j < n && code[j] is not ('{' or ';' or '\n')) j++;
                if (At(code, j) == '{')
                {
                    Wrap(sb, "selector", code.AsSpan(i, j - i));
                    i = j;
                    continue;
                }
            }

            if (IsAsciiLetter(ch) || ch == '-')
            {
                var j = i;
                while (j < n && (char.IsAsciiLetterOrDigit(code[j]) || code[j] == '-')) j++;
                var k = j;
                while (k < n && code[k] == ' ') k++;
                if (At(code, k) == ':' && At(code, k + 1) != ':')
                {
                    Wrap(sb, "property", code.AsSpan(i, j - i));
                }
                else
                {
                    Escape(sb, code.AsSpan(i, j - i));
                }
                i = j;
                continue;
            }

            Escape(sb, [ch]);
            i++;
        }

        return sb.ToString();
    }
}


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: tools/ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor/SyntaxHighlightingExtension.cs
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
using System.Text;
using Markdig;
using Markdig.Renderers;
using Markdig.Renderers.Html;
using Markdig.Syntax;

namespace ObserverMagazine.ContentProcessor;

public sealed class SyntaxHighlightingExtension : IMarkdownExtension
{
    public void Setup(MarkdownPipelineBuilder pipeline)
    {
    }

    public void Setup(MarkdownPipeline pipeline, IMarkdownRenderer renderer)
    {
        if (renderer is not HtmlRenderer html)
        {
            return;
        }

        var original = html.ObjectRenderers.FindExact<CodeBlockRenderer>();
        if (original is not null)
        {
            html.ObjectRenderers.Remove(original);
        }

        html.ObjectRenderers.Add(new HighlightingCodeBlockRenderer(original ?? new CodeBlockRenderer()));
    }

    private sealed class HighlightingCodeBlockRenderer(CodeBlockRenderer fallback) : HtmlObjectRenderer<CodeBlock>
    {
        protected override void Write(HtmlRenderer renderer, CodeBlock obj)
        {
            var language = (obj as FencedCodeBlock)?.Info ?? "";

            if (!renderer.EnableHtmlForBlock || fallback.BlocksAsDiv.Contains(language))
            {
                fallback.Write(renderer, obj);
                return;
            }

            var lines = obj.Lines;
            var code = new StringBuilder();
            for (var i = 0; i < lines.Count; i++)
            {
                code.Append(lines.Lines[i].Slice.ToString()).Append('\n');
            }

            renderer.EnsureLine();
            renderer.Write("<pre><code");
            renderer.WriteAttributes(obj);
            renderer.Write(">");
            renderer.Write(SyntaxHighlighter.Highlight(code.ToString(), language));
            renderer.WriteLine("</code></pre>");
        }
    }
}
